\noindent
我们在两个层面考察的每个替代方案，都达不到本节所立的设计目标，失去某条半格律、可替换性，或由构造保证的可扩展性；早期绑定与动态作用域失去最后这一条，因为它们让函数代替 mixin 留在原处，而函数体只在其参数处可扩展。

我们通过尝试本节中的每个替代方案并发现其失败，从而得到了这一语义：优先级合并，无论施于嵌套结构还是叶子，都引入了顺序依赖；早期绑定在组合能够提供引用目标之前就将引用冻结；动态作用域破坏了 $\lambda$-演算嵌入所依赖的替换；单目标 $\this$ 拒绝了可扩展性所要求的多目标组合。深度合并加上多目标迟绑定 $\this$，正是最终剩下的选择。
\else
% 作者侧（用户 2026-10-20）:不说「前四条」("这既不是for all也不是for some命题。就直接说五个"),设计目标是由构造满足五条标准的整体。
Section~\ref{sec:modularity} reads Meyer's five criteria as properties of composition and proves them
for inheritance-calculus. We adopt meeting all five by construction as the design goal, and ask whether
an alternative design could meet it by surveying every candidate known to us.

\paragraph{\bilingual{Degrees of freedom}{自由度}}
The analysis below assumes that the AST is a \emph{named tree}:
every edge carries a label, so that every
node is uniquely identified by the path from the root.
For configuration languages this is naturally the case: every
key in a JSON, YAML, or Nix attribute set is a label, and nesting
produces paths.%
\footnote{
  Nix is a functional language with first-class functions;
  its attribute sets alone do not form a named-tree DSL.
  The NixOS module system~\cite{nixos-contributors-nixos-modules}, however,
  restricts the user-facing interface to nested attribute sets
  composed by recursive merging, and it is this DSL-level
  structure that naturally forms a named tree.
}
For mainstream programming languages, such as
Java~\cite{gosling2015-java-se8-specification},
Haskell~\cite{marlow2010-haskell-report}, and
Scala~\cite{odersky2004-scala-overview}, the AST is not directly a
named tree, since anonymous expression positions and implicit
scopes lack labels.%
\footnote{
  For example, the body of a $\mathbf{let}$.
}
Such positions can nonetheless be given synthetic fresh labels.%
\footnote{
  Just as a compiler synthesizes a name for a Java anonymous class.
}
The forward translation of Section~\ref{sec:forward-translation}
does exactly this, giving anonymous positions synthetic labels
such as $\olbl{__result}$ and $\olbl{__call}$, so that every node
acquires a unique path and the AST becomes a named tree.

Under this premise, every language whose programs are such
named trees with references can be characterized by two finite sets:
a set~$\mathcal{N}$ of \emph{node types}, each determining how a
node organizes its subtree, and a set~$\mathcal{R}$ of
\emph{reference types}, each determining how one position in the
tree refers to another.
The syntax is fixed by the choice of $\mathcal{N}$ and
$\mathcal{R}$; the semantics is determined by how each node type
structures composition and how each reference type resolves its
target.

{\small\typewriterInlineCodeInHtml
  \begin{center}
    \begin{tabular}{L{0.2\columnwidth}L{0.36\columnwidth}L{0.32\columnwidth}}
      \textbf{Language}
      & \textbf{Node types $\mathcal{N}$}
      & \textbf{Reference types $\mathcal{R}$} \\
      \hline
      Java (OOP)
      & class, interface, method,
      block, conditional, loop,
      \mintinline{java}{throw}, lambda, \ldots
      & variable, field access, method call,
      \mintinline{java}{extends}/\mintinline{java}{implements},
      \mintinline{java}{import}, \ldots \\[3pt]
      Haskell (FP)
      & $\lambda$, application, \mintinline{haskell}{let},
      \mintinline{haskell}{case}, \mintinline{haskell}{data},
      type class, instance, \ldots
      & variable, constructor,
      type class dispatch,
      \mintinline{haskell}{import}, \ldots \\[3pt]
      Scala\footnote{
        Scala is a multi-paradigm language.
      }
      & class, trait, object, method,
      block, conditional, \mintinline{scala}{match},
      \mintinline{scala}{while}, \mintinline{scala}{throw}, \ldots
      & variable, member access,
      \mintinline{scala}{extends}, \mintinline{scala}{with},
      implicits, \mintinline{scala}{import}, \ldots \\
      \hline
      $\lambda$-calculus
      & abstraction, application
      & variable \\
      Inheritance-calculus
      & mixin, definition
      & reference \\
    \end{tabular}
  \end{center}
}


\paragraph{\bilingual{Substitution in a context}{上下文中的替换}}
Substitutability concerns inheritance: what inherits keeps every observation of what it inherits
(Theorem~\ref{thm:inheritance-substitutability}).
Replacing $T$ by a substitutable $S$ in a context is a different operation, and late binding separates
the two.
In the document \mintinline{yaml}{m: [t: [x: []], s: [[m, t]], u: [[t]]]} with
\mintinline{yaml}{k: [[m], t: [z: []]]}, the mixin \mintinline{yaml}{s} inherits
\mintinline{yaml}{t} and is substitutable for it, yet replacing the reference
\mintinline{yaml}{[t]} of \mintinline{yaml}{u} by \mintinline{yaml}{[s]} loses the path
$(\olbl{k}, \olbl{u}, \olbl{z})$: \mintinline{yaml}{u} reaches \mintinline{yaml}{t} through a
lexical reference, which late binding resolves to the override of \mintinline{yaml}{t} in
\mintinline{yaml}{k}, while \mintinline{yaml}{s} names $(\olbl{m}, \olbl{t})$ absolutely.
Replacing $T$ by $S$ loses no observation where the context reaches $T$ through absolute references
(Corollary~\ref{cor:behavior-compatibility}).

We now classify the design space to explain why we chose the
semantics of Section~\ref{sec:mixin-trees}.
Two axes remain free:
the \emph{composition mechanism}, which determines how the single reference
type incorporates definitions;
and the \emph{reference resolution}, which determines how the single reference
type finds its target.

\subsection*{\bilingual{The Composition Mechanism}{组合机制}}

% 作者侧(用户 2026-09-11 定向,2026-10-08 要求纳入本次修订):组合机制按「同名相遇处保留几个
% 贡献」一条三分(全部 = 深度合并 / 一个不留 = 冲突拒绝 / 留一个 = 优先级合并),逐构造、更一般地
% 逐条件施用。Shallow merge 不是独立类别,而是对嵌套结构取挑或拒的别名,故不单列。C/I/A 三列已删:
% 三律按语言取值,机制按构造取值,两者粒度不同,三律的成败改由下面各条目按策略交代。
% 作者侧:β-归约与条件并入不是同名相遇的策略,保留为三分之外的两个机制(代理决定,随 2026-10-08
% 的计划获用户批准)。
Every composition mechanism determines how the definitions of one
abstraction are incorporated into another.
We write $x \oplus y$ for the composition of two abstractions $x$
and~$y$.
Mechanisms differ at a \emph{same-label meeting}, where $x$ and $y$
both define one label, and we classify the strategies for such a
meeting by how many of its contributions survive:
\emph{deep merge} keeps all of them,
\emph{conflict rejection} keeps none and reports an error,
and \emph{priority merge} keeps one.
A language applies a strategy per construct, or more generally per
condition on the meeting definitions, such as the kind of construct,
an annotation, a typing judgment, whether a member is abstract, or
whether two types share a constructor; one language may therefore
take different strategies in different places.
A shallow merge is not a fourth strategy: it treats a nested
structure as a leaf and applies priority merge or conflict rejection
to it.
Two further mechanisms incorporate definitions without a same-label
meeting: $\beta$-reduction and conditional incorporation.

% 作者侧(用户 2026-10-08 问:「Java、Haskell和Scala这种过于流行的语言是不是就不需要citation了?但Nix就需要?」):
%   表中作为「代表」的语言是分类论断的证据,与 Nix 同等对待,一律引其语言定义:Haskell 引 Haskell 2010
%   Report,JS spread 引 ECMAScript 2018(对象展开首次入标准的版本;2017 版无 CopyDataProperties,2026-10-08 核)。
% 作者侧(用户 2026-10-09):"设计空间讨论时，不应该列出每一份工作，而是每一个表格每一行只列出单个典型例子"；
%   "具体的先前工作的相同和不同应该移到相关工作章节"。优先级合并只留 JS spread:一般读者最熟悉,又是纯粹的
%   优先级合并(Jsonnet 的 + 另带 +:)。β-归约只留 Haskell:函数应用就是它的组合机制(Java 另有继承)。
%   例子的选择是代理的判断。Jsonnet、Nix //、Java、Scala、Cardelli 1988 的比较归第 9 节。
{\small
\begin{center}
  \begin{tabular}{llL{0.17\textwidth}L{0.3\textwidth}}
    \bilingual{\textbf{Mechanism}}{\textbf{机制}}
    & \bilingual{\textbf{Kept at a meeting}}{\textbf{相遇时保留}}
    & \bilingual{\textbf{Extensibility}}{\textbf{可扩展性}}
    & \bilingual{\textbf{Representative}}{\textbf{代表}} \\
    \hline
    \bilingual{Deep merge}{深度合并}
    & \bilingual{all}{全部}
    & \bilingual{universal}{通用} & \bilingual{this paper}{本文} \\
    \bilingual{Conflict rejection}{冲突拒绝}
    & \bilingual{none}{无}
    & \bilingual{none on overlap}{重叠时无扩展}
    & Harper--Pierce \\
    \bilingual{Priority merge}{优先级合并}
    & \bilingual{one}{一个}
    & \bilingual{universal}{通用}
    & JS spread \\
    \hline
    $\beta$-\bilingual{reduction}{归约}
    & ---
    & \bilingual{opt-in}{按需选择} & Haskell \\
    \bilingual{Conditional incorporation}{条件并入}
    & ---
    & \bilingual{causes divergence}{导致发散} & --- \\
  \end{tabular}
\end{center}
}

\begin{description}
  % 作者侧:深度合并有两种实现(cheatsheet「§8 分类法的修订备料」):结构决定(递归进子树)与随标签
  % 提供组合函数(叶子上保住深度合并的办法,例:MIXINv2 的 @merge 收到全部 patch,签名
  % Callable[[Iterator[TPatch]], TResult],见 packages/mixinv2/src/mixinv2/_core.py 的 merge,
  % 2026-10-08 核)。这一格说明深度合并递归到叶子时,叶子上的同名贡献也有全部保留的办法。
  \item[Deep merge]
    Merging same-label definitions recurses into their subtrees
    ($\overrides$, equation~\ref{eq:overrides}).
    Every label is an extension point, whether or not the
    original author anticipated extension.
    Recursion needs a subtree to recurse into.
    At a leaf, a construct without one, deep merge needs a function
    that combines the contributions, supplied with the label:
    in MIXINv2, a \mintinline{python}{@merge} definition receives every
    patch to its label rather than the last of them, and the
    documented example collects the patches into a set.
    In inheritance-calculus every definition binds a label to a
    mixin, which is itself a tree, so the recursion alone suffices.

  % 作者侧:冲突拒绝不得写成「三律空洞成立」:这一框架可以下移一层,同样施于深度合并自身的三律。
  % 重叠时拒绝直接失幂等,这句不受此影响,因为深度合并是全域的。
  % 不匹配时拒绝(例如两个类型的构造子不同)永不在 x ⊕ x 处触发,幂等无损。原先以 Cardelli 1988 的 meet 为例
  % (cheatsheet「Cardelli 1988」条目,§13 的定义);按每行一个例子,本条只留 Harper--Pierce,Cardelli 1988 归第 9 节。
  \item[Conflict rejection]
    Same-label definitions are rejected as errors.
    When the rejection is triggered by an overlap, it rejects
    $x \oplus x$ whenever $x$ defines a label, so idempotence fails;
    and it prevents extending existing
    labels~\cite{harper1991-symmetric-record-concatenation}, precisely
    the composability that extensibility requires.
    When the rejection is triggered by a mismatch instead, such as two
    types with different constructors, it never fires on
    $x \oplus x$.

  \item[Priority merge]
    One source takes precedence,
    so $x \oplus y$ and $y \oplus x$ differ when $x$ and $y$ define
    one label differently, introducing an ordering dependency:
    the strategy reads which operand a contribution comes from, and
    commutativity fails.
    JS spread~\cite{ecma2018-ecmascript} applies it to a nested
    object as well, replacing the nested object whole.

  \item[$\beta$-reduction]
    Only the parameters placed by the author are extension
    points; the function body is closed.
    To make a new aspect extensible, the original function must
    be rewritten to accept an additional parameter.
    Any composition mechanism based on function application
    inherits this limitation, because $\beta$-reduction
    closes the function body.
    Extensibility in such languages is opt-in for the same reason.
    % 作者侧(用户 2026-10-17):由构造保证的可扩展性按定义不可被打破,所以不可模拟;访问者模式不能在两个维度上扩展,
    % 对象代数与 finally tagless 能,其局限是只在编码预先设计的维度上可扩展,在这些维度上扩展可能可以组合。
    % 用户点名了这两者,故本行破例举两个例子,不受"每行一个典型例子"的限制。
    Object algebras~\cite{oliveira2012-object-algebras} and finally
    tagless interpreters~\cite{carette2009-finally-tagless} work around
    this limitation in two dimensions, new data variants and new
    operations, which their encodings design in advance, and in those
    dimensions the extensions can compose; an aspect outside the
    designed dimensions stays closed.

  \item[Conditional incorporation]
    Removing or conditionally including definitions
    based on the presence of other definitions
    creates a dependency cycle:
    whether a definition exists depends on
    whether another definition exists, which in turn
    depends on the first.
    The recursive evaluation
    (Appendix~\ref{app:well-definedness}) cannot
    reach a fixed point when addition and removal
    alternate, causing divergence.
    This differs from the divergence inherent to
    Turing completeness: Turing-complete programs
    diverge because computation is unbounded, whereas
    conditional incorporation diverges because the
    immediate consequence operator $T_P$ oscillates
    rather than ascending monotonically.
\end{description}

% 作者侧:只写两条已论证的失败机制(留一个即失交换;重叠时拒即失幂等),不写 cheatsheet「由此得到
% 的中心陈述」里「三律成立当且仅当……」的 iff:没有证明(代理决定,随 2026-10-08 的计划获批)。
% 「唯一一个」这句实质不变,只把主语从 candidates 改为 mechanisms,与新分类一致。
\noindent
A language thus fixes a strategy for each construct or condition,
and each place where it takes priority merge, or a conflict
rejection triggered by an overlap, is a place where commutativity
or idempotence can fail.
Inheritance-calculus takes deep merge at every same-label meeting.
Among the mechanisms above, deep merge is the only one we found
that is commutative, idempotent, associative
(Section~\ref{sec:modularity}), and
gives a substitutable inheritance relation
(Theorem~\ref{thm:inheritance-substitutability}).
The other mechanisms each break substitutability, leave paths
nonextensible, or leave evaluation without termination.

\subsection*{\bilingual{The Reference Resolution}{引用解析}}

Once deep merge over mixins is fixed as the composition
mechanism, three of the five semantic equations are determined
by the syntax:
$\bases$~(\ref{eq:bases}) reads its inheritance
sources, $\supers$~(\ref{eq:supers}) takes the transitive
closure, and $\overrides$~(\ref{eq:overrides}) implements
the merge.
The only remaining degree of freedom is how references
resolve: the $\resolve$~(\ref{eq:resolve}) and
$\this$~(\ref{eq:this}) equations.
We examine the three known candidates.

\begin{description}
  \item[Early binding]
    % 作者侧(用户 2026-10-17):"这两条能不能用recall的方式来改写？明确使用“recall”这个词。"
    %   现象出自 Cook 1989 第 2.3 节第 12 页的 naive derivation(原文已核对);Cook 不用 early binding
    %   这个词,名字是本文的。
    We recall from Cook~\cite{cook1989-denotational-inheritance} that a
    derivation which leaves self-reference unchanged still refers to the
    parts of the original structure that should have been modified.
    Early binding leaves references this way: they resolve at
    definition time to a fixed path, independently of how the mixin is
    later inherited.
    % 作者侧(2026-10-09,用户):"以实测为准，把假话改掉"。原例 CUE 与实测不符:CUE 的引用在合一之后读到
    %   另一操作数给出的值,是单目标晚绑定(inheritance-calculus-related-work-survey/witnesses/
    %   的 w1、w2)。改用 Nix 的 rec 加 //,实测见同目录 n1_rec_update.nix:覆盖 a 之后 b 与 inner.r 仍为 1。
    Nix's recursive attribute sets~\cite{dolstra2010-nixos} exemplify
    this: a reference inside \mintinline{nix}{rec} resolves to an
    attribute of the same set when the set is defined, so after the
    update operator \mintinline{nix}{//} overrides that attribute, the
    reference still reads the old value, and so does a reference in a
    nested set.
    This prevents the mixin from serving as a call stack for the
    $\lambda$-calculus: $\beta$-reduction requires the callee to
    observe the argument supplied at the call site, which is a
    later composition.
    With early binding, references are frozen before this
    composition occurs.
    In the equations,
    $\resolve$~(\ref{eq:resolve}) calls
    $\this$~(\ref{eq:this}), which walks the inherited
    tree structure; late binding is what allows
    $\this$ to see paths contributed by later-composed
    mixins.
    Without a Turing-complete mixin, functions must be
    reintroduced as the composition mechanism, re-exposing
    the opt-in extensibility problem above.

  \item[Dynamic scope]
    % 作者侧(用户 2026-10-17):同上,以 recall 改写。两处引文出自 AI Memo 453:第一处在第 20 页动态
    %   作用域的定义处,第二处在摘要(扫描件 OCR 后逐字核对)。Moses 1970 只区分绑定环境与激活环境,
    %   不作评判,且已被 Steele--Sussman 第 20 页引用,故不另引。
    Dynamic scope resolves references at the point of use rather than
    the definition site.
    We recall from Steele and
    Sussman~\cite{steele1978-art-of-the-interpreter} that dynamic scope
    makes free variable references ``refer to the bindings of variables
    in other procedures higher up in the chain of calls'', and that it
    is ``unsuitable for constructing procedural abstractions''.
    % 作者侧(2026-10-09,用户):"以实测为准，把假话改掉"。原例 Jsonnet 与实测不符:p 的方法在 q 里调用时
    %   self 仍是 p,复制出去的嵌套对象仍看原来的外层(witnesses/ 的 j2、j3、j4),是单目标晚绑定。改用
    %   OmegaConf 的插值,实测见同目录 o1_relative_interpolation.py(omegaconf 2.3.0):节点放到另一个根下,
    %   相对插值与绝对插值都读新根的字段。
    The interpolations of OmegaConf~\cite{yadan2018-omegaconf}, the
    configuration library of Hydra~\cite{yadan2023-hydra}, follow
    this approach: an interpolation, absolute like
    \texttt{\$\{outer\}} or relative like \texttt{\$\{..outer\}},
    resolves against the configuration in which its node is used, so
    a node placed under another root reads the field of that root,
    not the field of the configuration that defined the node.
    Dynamic scope breaks the $\lambda$-calculus embedding
    of Section~\ref{sec:forward-translation}.
    The Lookup Lemma (Lemma~\ref{lem:lookup})
    relies on the separation of binders:
    the de~Bruijn index of a variable $y \neq x$ climbs from the
    definition-site path to the scope binding~$y$, a scope
    level different from~$x$'s binder, so overriding
    \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{__parameter: |$\translationOfV$|} at~$x$'s redex
    has no effect on the resolution of~$y$.
    With dynamic scope this separation fails.
    As with early binding, the mixin cannot serve as a complete
    replacement for functions.

  \item[Single-target $\this$]
    \begin{sloppypar}
    In Cook's semantics, a late-bound reference has one target, since an object is the fixed point of
    one generator~\cite{cook1989-denotational-inheritance}.
    Single-target $\this$ is a promising candidate:
    the Single-Path Lemma (Lemma~\ref{lem:single-path}) shows that
    for translated $\lambda$-terms, at every resolution step the
    convergence observation depends on, the frontier set~$S$ in
    $\this$~(\ref{eq:this}) contains at most one path,
    so the $\lambda$-calculus embedding does not require
    multi-target resolution.
    However, single-target $\this$ misses the design goal for a
    different reason.
    When multiple inheritance routes reach the same scope,
    single-target resolution must reject the situation or select
    one route.
    % 作者侧（底层论证，作者批准 2026-07-02，正文只留一句）：对称钻石上（把两基重命名对调的
    %   自同构），单目标结果若对置换不变，就无法落在被对调的两个位点之一；而重结合不变的
    %   只有解析集合的保留字子树外部分（cor:merge-this-preservation），位点本身含分组脚手架，
    %   故不存在既不读顺序也不读分组的典范选择。这一句把单目标之恶从「扩展性论证」升级为
    %   「三律论证」：多目标不是锦上添花，而是三律在全函数组合下的必然形态。
    % 作者侧(2026-10-08):原句称拒绝「让三律只空洞地成立」,与组合机制小节同一框架,已删;此处只
    %   陈述拒绝的后果(嵌套扩展被拒),不就三律下断言。
    A selection must read some asymmetry: reading reference order
    surfaces as a failure of commutativity, and reading the grouping
    structure surfaces as a failure of associativity.
    A rejection refuses the composition instead.
    The NixOS module system~\cite{nixos-contributors-nixos-modules} raises a
    duplicate-declaration error
    (Appendix~\ref{app:scala-multi-target}).
    This rejects nested extensions where two independently authored
    modules define the same option, a composition that
    Section~\ref{sec:case-study} demonstrates.
    \end{sloppypar}

    Our first three implementations of MIXINv2 used
    standard techniques for $\this$ resolution: closure-based
    fixed points following Cook~\cite{cook1989-denotational-inheritance}, and
    stack-based environment lookup using de~Bruijn
    indices~\cite{debruijn1972-nameless-dummies}.
    All three produced subtle bugs when inheritance introduced
    multiple routes to the same enclosing scope.
    The difficulty was structural.
    A closure captures a single environment, so a fixed-point
    combinator solves for a single $\this$ target.
    A stack-based environment is a linear chain in which each scope
    has a single enclosing scope.
    Both data structures embed a single-valued assumption
    incompatible with the multi-target situation that multiple
    inheritance naturally produces
    (Appendix~\ref{app:scala-multi-target}).
    Equation~(\ref{eq:this}) emerged from abandoning this
    assumption: it tracks a \emph{frontier set}~$S$ rather than a
    single current scope.
\end{description}

\noindent
The following table summarizes the vulnerabilities of all
alternatives at both levels.

    & \bilingual{\textbf{Representative}}{\textbf{代表}} \\
    \hline
    \multirow{4}{*}{\rotatebox[origin=c]{90}{\scriptsize \bilingual{composition}{组合}}}
    & \bilingual{Conflict rejection}{冲突拒绝}
    & \bilingual{self-composition and same-label extensions rejected}{自组合与同名扩展被拒绝}
    & Harper--Pierce \\
    & \bilingual{Priority merge}{优先级合并}
    % 作者侧(代理决定 2026-10-17,用户未否决):代表例子由 JS spread 改为线性化,与引言 P4 一致;用户 2026-10-18 指出
    %   C3 并非最早的线性化,改为 Flavors。"可能不存在顺序"见 Moon 1986 第 3 页 SPICE-CAKE 例。
    & \bilingual{ordering dependency; no order may exist}{顺序依赖;可能不存在顺序}
    & Flavors \\
    & $\beta$-\bilingual{reduction}{归约}
    & \bilingual{opt-in extensibility}{按需选择的可扩展性}
    & Haskell \\
    & \bilingual{Conditional incorporation}{条件并入}
    & \bilingual{causes divergence}{导致发散}
    & --- \\
    \hline
    \multirow{3}{*}{\rotatebox[origin=c]{90}{\scriptsize \bilingual{resolution}{解析}}}
    & \bilingual{Early binding}{早期绑定}
    & \bilingual{mixin cannot replace function}{mixin 无法替代函数}
    & Nix~\mintinline{nix}{rec} \\
    & \bilingual{Dynamic scope}{动态作用域}
    & \bilingual{mixin cannot replace function}{mixin 无法替代函数}
    & OmegaConf \\
    & \bilingual{Single-target $\this$}{单目标 $\this$}

\noindent
Each alternative we examined at either level misses the design goal
this section set out, losing a semilattice law, substitutability, or
extensibility by construction, which early binding and dynamic scope
lose by leaving functions, whose bodies are extensible only at their
parameters, in place of mixins.
% 作者侧（盲读第二轮,2026-10-20）:原先此处重新定义 modularity by construction(半格律等四条"连同"无副作用与覆盖,附脚注),
%   与引言的定义重复,又把保护性单列(用户:"protection没有任何特殊之处，不应该和其他四个分开");其后半句与上一段重复。整段删去,
%   代理的决定,用户见过提议未反对。旧文见 git show 7fd562d2e:MIXINv2/paper/inheritance-calculus.tex。

We arrived at this semantics by trying each alternative in this section
and finding that it failed:
priority merge, whether at nested structures or at leaves,
introduced ordering dependencies;
early binding froze references before composition could
supply them;
dynamic scope broke the substitution that the
$\lambda$-calculus embedding relies on;
single-target $\this$ rejected the multi-target compositions
that extensibility demands.
Deep merge with multi-target late-binding $\this$ is what
remained.
\fi

\subsection{\bilingual{Computational Models and \texorpdfstring{$\lambda$}{λ}-Calculus Semantics}{计算模型与 \texorpdfstring{$\lambda$}{λ}-演算语义}}

% 作者侧(代理提议,用户 2026-10-09 回复"同意"):第 9 节主线的第 1 条,本小节按「消去哪种原语」组织,见
%   inheritance-calculus-structure-ledger.md「第 9 节主线」。组合子逻辑消去的是约束变量(Schönfinkel 的
%   原话与 Quine 导言),Meyer 的组合代数里 λ-抽象是导出的、应用是原语;依据见调查批 7 的 D2。
\paragraph{\bilingual{Minimal Turing-complete models}{最小图灵完备模型}}
% 作者侧（去重,2026-10-24,已批准的计划 A:"极小 Turing 完备模型(压到一两句)"）:删去 $I = SKK$ 脚注、括号抽象的指数膨胀、
%   Meyer 之后"calibration runs toward the λ-calculus"与 Scott $D_\infty$ 一句、Dolan 的寻址模式细节与"no independent semantic
%   domain"半句;原文见提交说明。保留读者仍会问的区别:这些模型在已有模型的语义框架内达到图灵完备。
\ifPaperChinese
Sch\"onfinkel~\cite{schonfinkel1924-combinatory-logic} 与 Curry~\cite{curry1958-combinatory-logic} 把 $\lambda$-演算归约为组合子 $S$、$K$ 与 $I$,消除了约束变量,而应用仍是唯一的运算,Meyer~\cite{meyer1982-model-of-lambda-calculus} 证明了满足五个附加条件的组合代数恰好就是 $\lambda$-代数;Dolan~\cite{dolan2013-mov-turing-complete} 证明了 x86 的 \mintinline{nasm}{mov} 指令单独就已图灵完备,而不提供任何超越 RAM 机的抽象机制。这些模型中的每一个,都在某个已有模型的语义框架内实现了图灵完备性。
\else
Sch\"onfinkel~\cite{schonfinkel1924-combinatory-logic} and
Curry~\cite{curry1958-combinatory-logic} reduced the $\lambda$-calculus to the combinators $S$, $K$,
and $I$, eliminating bound variables while application remains the one operation, and
Meyer~\cite{meyer1982-model-of-lambda-calculus} showed that the combinatory algebras satisfying five
additional conditions are exactly the $\lambda$-algebras;
% 作者侧(2026-10-09):原有一句「图灵机缺乏随机访问」不在「消去哪种原语」的主线上,为字数预算删去(代理决定)。
Dolan~\cite{dolan2013-mov-turing-complete} showed that the x86 \mintinline{nasm}{mov} instruction
alone is Turing complete, providing no abstraction mechanisms beyond those of the RAM Machine.
Each of these models achieves Turing completeness within the semantic framework of an existing model.
\fi

%% === 审稿人备忘：SF-calculus 与 inheritance-calculus 的关系 ===
%%
%% Barry Jay 的 SF-calculus（Intensional Computation with Higher-Order
%% Functions, TCS 2019, doi:10.1016/j.tcs.2019.02.016；前身为
%% Confusion in the Church-Turing Thesis, arXiv:1410.7103, 2014）
%% 和 inheritance-calculus 都试图解决 λ-calculus 的 extensionality 限制，
%% 但方向完全不同。
%%
%% 共同出发点：λ-calculus 是 extensional 的——只能观察函数的输入输出行为，
%% 无法检查程序的内部结构。两个内部结构不同但行为相同的 λ 项不可区分。
%%   - Jay 的诊断：λ-calculus 缺乏 intensional 操作（检查项的结构），
%%     无法定义结构等式判定。
%%   - 本文的诊断：λ-calculus 的 function body 是 closed 的，无法从外部
%%     添加新的可观察投影（open extension）。
%%
%% 核心机制对比：
%%   SF-calculus：保留 S、K（或 λ），添加 F（factorisation）算子，
%%     可以将闭合范式拆解为组成部分。程序仍然是函数，只是多了检查函数
%%     结构的能力。属于 λ-calculus 的 *扩展*。
%%   Inheritance-calculus：用三个不同的原语（record、definition、
%%     inheritance）替代 λ-calculus 的三个原语。β-reduction 是 deep merge
%%     的特例（Section 3 的翻译 T 将函数应用映射为继承）。
%%     属于 λ-calculus 的 *包含*（subsumption）。
%%
%% 关于 F 算子的编码：
%%   F 做两件事：(1) 判断结构形态（atom vs. compound），
%%   (2) 提取子部分。
%%   在 inheritance-calculus 中：
%%     - 提取子部分是平凡的：所有值都是透明 record，通过 qualified this
%%       投影任意 slot 即可。Theorem 6 的 separating context 就在读取
%%       parameter slot（↑².parameter）。
%%     - 判断结构形态并分支：需要 Acceptance pattern（Section 4）。
%%       NatVisitor 对 Nat 做的事情与 F 对 SF-calculus 项做的事情
%%       完全对应：VisitZero/SuccessorVisitor 对应 F 的 atom/compound
%%       分支。Visitor 可以通过 open extension 添加到已有 term 上，
%%       不修改原始定义。
%%   Acceptance 在精神上就是 F：两者都是对 closed normal form 做
%%   constructor dispatch。区别在于 F 是不可扩展的 built-in primitive
%%   （硬编码了 S 和 F 两个 atom），而 Acceptance 是可扩展的 encoding
%%   （新增 constructor 只需继承新的 Accepted* 分支，即多维度可扩展性）。
%%
%% 精确差异：
%%   SF-calculus 把 intensional observation 做成了 object-level 的
%%   primitive reduction rule（F 是一个 term）；
%%   inheritance-calculus 把它留在了 meta-level（观察者总能通过
%%   supers 查询任何路径是否存在），而 term-level 的分支需要通过
%%   Acceptance pattern 显式编码。
%%   换言之：inheritance-calculus 中 F 的 *读取* 能力是免费的
%%   （所有值都是透明 record），但 F 的 *分支* 能力需要 Acceptance。
%%   SF-calculus 的 F 则把读取和分支捆绑在一个不可扩展的 primitive 里。
%%   Acceptance 是可扩展的（新增 constructor 只需继承新分支，
%%   即多维度可扩展性），而 F 硬编码了固定的 atom 集合
%%   （S 和 F）。
%%
%% 关于 Jay 所谓的 "trivial solution of the Halting Problem"：
%%   Jay 用非标准编码将每个程序（包括不停机的）映射到闭合范式（静态数据），
%%   然后结构等式判定变得平凡。这并不推翻图灵定理——它改变了问题的
%%   形式化方式（把程序当静态数据而非可执行代码），而非解决了原来的问题。
%%   Jay 的真正论点是：λ-definability 和 Turing computability 不是同一回事，
%%   Church-Turing Thesis 的等价性证明隐含了编码假设。
%%
%% 正文篇幅不够论述 embeds SF-calculus，暂不展开。
%% 如果审稿人提到 SF-calculus，可以基于上述分析撰写回复或补充段落。
%% ================================================================


% 作者侧（去重,2026-10-24,已批准的计划 A）:删去「递归方程与不动点语义」整段(van Emden--Kowalski、Aczel、Leroy--Grall、
%   tablambda)。依据用户的取舍标准:读者不会问这些工作与继承演算的区别,或第 4、5 节已回答;tablambda 用户:"不需要特殊为
%   tablambda 开后门","tablambda不是配套工作"。原文见提交说明,或 git show a62824899:MIXINv2/paper/inheritance-calculus.tex。
\paragraph{\bilingual{Graph and filter models}{图模型与滤子模型}}
% 作者侧(2026-10-08):一阶语义的首创性说法为假,本段之前有两句已删,不得复活任何「一阶指称语义首创」或「λ-演算
%   语义必需函数空间」的说法;图模型与滤子模型正是不构造函数空间的 λ-模型。事实只取一手核实过的:
%   BCD 1983 Def. 3.2(application)、Def. 3.4(按类型指派定义解释)、Thm 3.5(直接验证 λ-模型条件);
%   Dezani 等 1998 Thm 11.3([D→D] 是 D 的 retract);Berline 2000 只读到摘要(webbed models 是由
%   web 构造的幂集),故只引作综述。见 cheatsheet「λ-模型一线」。
%   按 Related Work house rule,只复述对方,不转到我们。
%   末句(2026-10-09)按第 9 节主线「消去哪种原语」:模型层把函数表示为集合,被解释的项仍以抽象与应用为原语(调查批 8)。
\ifPaperChinese
图模型与滤子模型为 $\lambda$-演算给出了载体为幂集的模型:Berline~\cite{berline2000-webbed-models} 综述了这类由 web 构造的 webbed 模型。在 Barendregt、Coppo 与 Dezani 的滤子模型~\cite{barendregt1983-filter-lambda-model} 中,一个项的解释是可指派给它的交类型之集,对滤子 $d_1$、$d_2$ 与类型 $\sigma$、$\tau$,应用是集合概括 $d_1 \cdot d_2 = \{\tau \mid \exists \sigma \in d_2.\ \sigma \to \tau \in d_1\}$,而 $\lambda$-模型的条件被直接验证,不构造函数空间。Dezani-Ciancaglini、Giovannetti 与 de'Liguoro~\cite{dezani1998-intersection-types-bohm-trees} 将函数空间作为载体的一个 retract 恢复出来,并把滤子模型与 Böhm 树联系起来。这些模型所解释的项保留抽象与应用作为原语。
\else
Graph models and filter models give the $\lambda$-calculus models
whose carrier is a powerset: Berline~\cite{berline2000-webbed-models}
surveys these webbed models, built from webs.
In the filter model of Barendregt, Coppo, and
Dezani~\cite{barendregt1983-filter-lambda-model}, the interpretation
of a term is the set of intersection types assignable to it;
for filters $d_1$, $d_2$ and types $\sigma$, $\tau$, application is the
set comprehension
$d_1 \cdot d_2 = \{\tau \mid \exists \sigma \in d_2.\ \sigma \to \tau \in d_1\}$,
and the conditions of a $\lambda$-model are verified directly,
without constructing a function space.
Dezani-Ciancaglini, Giovannetti, and
de'Liguoro~\cite{dezani1998-intersection-types-bohm-trees} recover the
function space as a retract of the carrier and relate filter models
to B\"ohm trees.
The terms these models interpret keep abstraction and application as
primitives.
\fi

% 作者侧(代理提议,用户 2026-10-09 回复"同意"):本小节是第 9 节主线的第 2 条,见
%   inheritance-calculus-structure-ledger.md「第 9 节主线」。主线:方法体由规则或方程取代,但规则或方程是
%   对象之外的第二种形式体系,关系是原语。事实全部取自 inheritance-calculus-related-work-survey/
%   batch-2-rule-languages.md;只复述对方,句子止于其局限,不转到本文。
\subsection{\bilingual{Rule Languages with Objects}{带对象的规则语言}}

\paragraph{\bilingual{Objects in deductive languages}{演绎语言中的对象}}
% 作者侧(2026-10-09):F-logic 读了一手全文的 §1、§3、§4、§7.3、§14、§15(JACM 预印本);IQL 两句原在「递归方程与
%   不动点语义」段,按主线移到此处,并补「把方法留在模型之外」(技术报告 §1 p.7);Soufflé 只读了官方文档四页。
%   IQL 靠新增规则扩展。
\ifPaperChinese
Kifer、Lausen 与 Wu 的 F-logic~\cite{kifer1995-frame-logic} 把类、属性与方法作为 id-项放在与个体对象相同的论域中,而一个方法的行为由演绎规则定义,一个模块是逻辑之外的元注解。从多个超类继承来的签名相加,一个方法的类型是它们的类型之交;一个显式定义覆写一个继承来的值,而当两个超类提供冲突的值时,结果取决于先进行哪一步继承,且语义接受多个典范模型。Abiteboul 与 Kanellakis~\cite{abiteboul1989-object-identity-query-primitive, abiteboul1998-object-identity-jacm} 在 IQL 中以对象标识扩展了基于规则的查询语言;IQL 是带否定的膨胀式 Datalog,具有集合与元组类型、对象标识符的生成与弱赋值,在复制消除的意义下完备,并把方法、封装与方法继承留在其模型之外;一个对象标识符的值只在其未定义时被赋值一次,因此 IQL 程序靠新增规则而扩展,而非靠在已有对象上新增成员。Datalog 引擎 Souffl\'e 的组件~\cite{souffle-contributors-components} 把关系、规则、事实与嵌套组件组织在一起;一个子组件接收其超组件的每一个元素,因此一个关系的事实与规则累积起来,而 \texttt{.override} 丢弃超组件声明为可覆写的关系的继承来的事实与规则。一个组件在编译时被展平为带前缀的关系的命名空间。在这三种语言中,规则取代了方法体,作为它们所描述的对象或组件之外的一种形式体系,而关系是原语。按第~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}~节的分类,F-logic 对继承的值、Soufflé 对可覆写的关系取优先级合并,IQL 加入否定;Soufflé 在实例化处才解析一个组件未声明的关系,这是动态作用域。
\else
Kifer, Lausen, and Wu's F-logic~\cite{kifer1995-frame-logic} treats
classes, attributes, and methods as id-terms in the same domain as
individual objects, while the behaviour of a method is defined by
deductive rules and a module is a meta-annotation outside the logic.
Signatures inherited from several superclasses add up, and a method's
type is the intersection of their types; an explicit definition
overrides an inherited value, and when two superclasses supply
conflicting values, the result depends on which inheritance step is
taken first, and the semantics accepts several canonical models.
Abiteboul and
Kanellakis~\cite{abiteboul1989-object-identity-query-primitive, abiteboul1998-object-identity-jacm}
extend rule-based query languages with object identity in IQL, an
inflationary Datalog with negation, set and tuple types, object
identifier invention, and weak assignment, which is complete up to
copy elimination and leaves methods, encapsulation, and method
inheritance outside its model; the value of an object identifier is
assigned once, while it is undefined, so an IQL program is extended by
new rules, not by new members on existing objects.
The components of the Datalog engine
Souffl\'e~\cite{souffle-contributors-components} group relations,
rules, facts, and nested components; a sub-component receives every
element of its super-components, so the facts and rules of a relation
accumulate, and \texttt{.override} discards the inherited facts and
rules of a relation that the super-component declared overridable.
A component is flattened at compile time into a namespace of prefixed
relations.
In these three languages, rules take the place of method bodies, as a
formalism apart from the objects or components they describe, and
relations are primitive.
In the taxonomy of Section~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}, F-logic takes priority merge for inherited
values and Souffl\'e for overridable relations, IQL adds negation, and Souffl\'e resolves a relation
that a component does not declare at instantiation, which is dynamic scope.
\fi

\paragraph{\bilingual{Object-oriented attribute grammars}{面向对象属性文法}}
% 作者侧(2026-10-09):Hedin 1989 只读到摘要与 Lund 的研究页(正文未取得),句子只用摘要里的说法:单继承分类、
%   方程覆盖、多重继承的问题。JastAdd 读了 SCP 版的重印本全文(§1、§4、§5.1、§5.2)。
\ifPaperChinese
Hedin 的面向对象属性文法~\cite{hedin1989-object-oriented-attribute-grammars} 在非终结符与产生式的单继承分类之上以属性与方程规约一个语言,其中子类覆写一个方程,并讨论了多重继承会引起的问题。JastAdd~\cite{hedin2003-jastadd} 把这样的方程与普通的 Java 方法结合起来:一个综合属性被翻译为一个抽象 Java 方法,由其方程实现,而一个方程的 self 是 Java 的 \texttt{this}。
\else
Hedin's object-oriented attribute
grammars~\cite{hedin1989-object-oriented-attribute-grammars} specify a
language by attributes and equations over a single-inheritance
classification of nonterminals and productions, in which a subclass
overrides an equation, and discuss the problems that multiple
inheritance would raise.
JastAdd~\cite{hedin2003-jastadd} combines such equations with ordinary
Java methods: a synthesized attribute is translated to an abstract
Java method, which its equations implement, and the self of an
equation is Java's \texttt{this}.
\fi

\paragraph{\bilingual{Feature structures}{特征结构}}
% 作者侧(2026-10-09):LOGIN 的一手来源未取得(ScienceDirect 拒绝访问),只有标题与 F-logic §15.1、Carpenter 第 14 章
%   摘要的二手描述,故第一句写明是谁的描述。LIFE 只有二手来源,代理决定不引。Carpenter 只读了书的简介与各章
%   Summary(Cambridge Core)。
\ifPaperChinese
LOGIN~\cite{ait-kaci1986-login} 把继承内建进逻辑编程:按 Kifer 等人~\cite{kifer1995-frame-logic} 与 Carpenter~\cite{carpenter1992-typed-feature-structures} 的描述,它把定子句的一阶项替换为 $\psi$-项,而 $\psi$-项的合一纳入了结构继承。Carpenter 发展了类型化特征结构,其类型构成一个允许多重继承的继承层次;两个特征结构只在其类型于该层次中相容时才合一,而定子句程序是它们之上的短语结构文法的一个特例。在两者中,这些结构之上的定子句取代了方法。
\else
LOGIN~\cite{ait-kaci1986-login} builds inheritance into logic
programming: as Kifer et al.~\cite{kifer1995-frame-logic} and
Carpenter~\cite{carpenter1992-typed-feature-structures} describe it,
it replaces the first-order terms of definite clauses with
$\psi$-terms, whose unification incorporates structural inheritance.
Carpenter develops typed feature structures, whose types form an
inheritance hierarchy that admits multiple inheritance; two feature
structures unify only when their types are compatible in that
hierarchy, and definite clause programs are a special case of phrase
structure grammars over them.
In both, definite clauses over these structures take the place of
methods.
\fi

\subsection{\bilingual{Inheritance, Objects, and Records}{继承、对象与记录}}

% 作者侧（去重,2026-10-24,已批准的计划 A）:删去三整段。「继承的指称语义」(Cook):单目标的来源已移入讨论「Single-target this」
%   一项(计划 C),其余读者不会问。「Mixin 与 trait」:第 2 节与表 1 已给出 mixin、C3、trait 的定律与冲突拒绝;代理提议
%   表 1 的扇形加图例足以承载各组合运算的律,用户:"我觉得行"。「泛型函数与开放类」(CLOS、MultiJava):第 2.4 节已有开放方法,MultiJava 的
%   代价按用户"如果留半句的话，能不能移到第二节？"移入第 2.4 节(计划 B)。原文见提交说明。
\paragraph{\bilingual{Object and record calculi}{对象演算与记录演算}}
\ifPaperChinese
Boudol~\cite{boudol2004-recursive-record-semantics} 证明了自引用记录需要一个不安全的不动点算子,其适定性依赖于求值策略。Cardelli~\cite{cardelli1992-extensible-records} 研究了带子类型的可扩展记录;Rémy~\cite{remy1989-row-polymorphism} 为 ML 中的记录和变体引入了行多态。这些演算全都在\emph{平坦}记录上操作:没有嵌套结构的递归合并,没有惰性观察,$\this$ 也需要一个显式的不动点组合子。Cardelli 的多重继承语义~\cite{cardelli1988-multiple-inheritance-semantics} 逐字段地对记录类型取 meet,并让构造子不同的类型的 meet 无定义;self 是类型固定的闭合值递归,没有晚绑定,而超类方法的访问“cannot be simulated in any general and direct way”。Wand~\cite{wand1991-record-concatenation-inference} 为一种右偏的记录连接做类型推导,这是优先级合并,self 是一个显式参数,晚绑定到单一目标。Self~\cite{ungar1987-self-language} 把类与对象合为一个 prototype、把字段与方法合为一个 slot,建立在可变状态之上;一个方法是一个除 slot 之外还带有代码的对象。Scala~\cite{odersky2004-scala-overview} 没有静态成员:一个类的共享成员放在其伴生 object 中,而 class、trait、object 仍是三种构造。
\else
\begin{sloppypar}
Boudol~\cite{boudol2004-recursive-record-semantics} showed that self-referential
records require an unsafe fixed-point operator whose
well-definedness depends on the evaluation strategy.
% 作者侧（去重,2026-10-24,已批准的计划 A）:删去 Harper--Pierce 的对称连接一句与 Abadi--Cardelli 两句,第 2 节与表 1 已有;
%   原文见提交说明。
Cardelli~\cite{cardelli1992-extensible-records} studied extensible records
with subtyping;
R\'emy~\cite{remy1989-row-polymorphism} introduced row polymorphism for
records and variants in ML.
All of these calculi operate on \emph{flat} records: there is
no recursive merging of nested structure, no lazy observation,
and $\this$ requires an explicit fixed-point combinator.
% 作者侧(2026-10-08):Cardelli 1988 与 Wand 1991 是多重继承语义一线的先前工作;事实取自 inheritance-calculus-prior-work-english.md 的一手核实(§13 的 meet、§3 的引文;
%   Wand 的右偏连接与显式 self)。放在「平坦记录」一句之后,因为 Cardelli 1988 的 meet 是递归的。
Cardelli's semantics of multiple
inheritance~\cite{cardelli1988-multiple-inheritance-semantics} meets
record types field by field and leaves the meet undefined on types
of different constructors; self is closed value recursion with a
fixed type, without late binding, and superclass method access
``cannot be simulated in any general and direct way''.
Wand~\cite{wand1991-record-concatenation-inference} infers types for a
record concatenation that is right-biased, a priority merge, with self an explicit parameter bound
late to a single target.
% 作者侧(2026-10-09):Self 的方法对象一句按第 9 节主线第 3 条补上,原话见 1991 年修订版 §6("in the case of methods,
%   also include code"),调查批 1。
Self~\cite{ungar1987-self-language} makes a class and an object one
prototype, and a field and a method one slot, over mutable state;
a method is an object that carries code besides its slots.
Scala~\cite{odersky2004-scala-overview} has no static members: a
class's shared members live in a companion object, while class,
trait, and object remain three constructs.
\end{sloppypar}
\fi

\paragraph{\bilingual{Virtual classes, family polymorphism, and DOT}{虚类、族多态与 DOT}}
% 作者侧(2026-10-09):BETA 的 do-part 一句按第 9 节主线第 3 条补上,原话见 BETA 书 §3.1 p.29("The do-part is an
%   imperative that describes the actions to be performed when the object is executed"),调查批 1。
% 作者侧(2026-10-08):BETA 一线是与本文最接近的先前工作;事实取自
%   cheatsheet「BETA 1983」「Madsen & M-P 1989」「gbeta」「虚类演算 2006」(BETA 书 §6.8 p.103 原话,
%   2026-09-11 取自 beta.cs.au.dk)。只复述对方,句子止于其局限。
\ifPaperChinese
BETA~\cite{kristensen1983-beta-abstraction-mechanisms} 把类、类型、过程、函数与泛型包统一在 pattern 之中,而 pattern 与对象仍然分开,pattern 属性与引用属性是两种声明形式;一个 pattern 的动作是它的 do-part,即一串命令式语句。虚 pattern 沿单一的超 pattern 链经进一步绑定而被扩展:其设计者“due to the lack of a profound theoretical understanding”而把多重继承排除在 BETA 之外,并补充说超类的顺序应当无关紧要~\cite[p.~103]{madsen1993-beta-programming-language}。Madsen 与 M{\o}ller-Pedersen~\cite{madsen1989-virtual-classes} 提出了虚类,虚类沿单一的超类链被扩展,没有二元组合算子。在 gbeta~\cite{ernst1999-gbeta, ernst2001-family-polymorphism} 中,Ernst 把一个族的扩展传播进其嵌套虚类,并引入了族多态,而方法按 C3 线性化排序,当两个操作数要求相反的顺序时组合在运行期失败。Ernst、Ostermann 与 Cook~\cite{ernst2006-virtual-class-calculus} 在虚类演算中形式化了族多态,其中类通过路径表达式 \texttt{this.out.C} 来访问,而线性化让最右的操作数胜出,因此组合不可交换。Amin 等人~\cite{amin2016-dependent-object-types} 在 DOT 演算中形式化了路径依赖类型,这是 Scala 的理论基础。在沿每条路线解析外层类这一点上,J\&~\cite{nystrom2006-nested-intersection} 最为接近:它建立在 Jx~\cite{nystrom2004-nested-inheritance} 的前缀类型之上(前缀类型命名一个被继承进新容器的嵌套类的外层类),以嵌套交集组合族,于是一个继承分属多个容器的嵌套类的类,以这些容器的交为其前缀。\footnote{其技术报告对前缀的形式定义~\cite[图~12]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection-report}只在两个容器都再绑定第三个容器的同一嵌套类时才把它们联系起来,所以对写在这些容器的共同超类中的名字,它给出的前缀只是该超类本身,与报告为之声明的性质相反~\cite[附录~A.3]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection-report};其发布的编译器则收集所有是该超类之子类型的容器。}它的组合不是深度合并:同名的嵌套类只有在其名字由共同祖先引入时才取交,而两个方法覆写同一方法的交集在子类覆写它之前是抽象的~\cite[第~3.3~节]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection}。它的实现既不接受循环继承,也不接受作为其容器之子类型的嵌套类,所以单凭嵌套类不能做一般计算;计算留在方法体中,而一个方法覆写另一个方法时执行哪个方法体,其形式语义不作规定~\cite[附录~A]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection-report};在运行时一个引用指向单一对象。在 gbeta、虚类演算与 DOT 中,每条路径都解析为\emph{单一}封闭对象中的\emph{单一}类;它们均未提供集合值解析的机制,而 Scala 在编译时拒绝多目标情形(附录~\ref{app:scala-multi-target})。
\else
BETA~\cite{kristensen1983-beta-abstraction-mechanisms} unifies
classes, types, procedures, functions, and generic packages in the
pattern, while patterns and objects stay apart and pattern and
reference attributes are two forms of declaration; the actions of a
pattern are its do-part, a sequence of imperative statements.
A virtual pattern is extended by further binding along a single
superpattern chain: its designers left multiple inheritance out of
BETA ``due to the lack of a profound theoretical understanding'',
adding that the order of the superclasses should be
insignificant~\cite[p.~103]{madsen1993-beta-programming-language}.
Madsen and M{\o}ller-Pedersen~\cite{madsen1989-virtual-classes}
present virtual classes, which are extended along a single superclass
chain, with no binary composition operator.
In gbeta~\cite{ernst1999-gbeta, ernst2001-family-polymorphism}, Ernst
propagates the extension of a family into its nested virtual classes
and introduces family polymorphism, while methods are ordered by C3
linearization and a composition fails at run time when its two
operands require opposite orders.
Ernst, Ostermann, and Cook~\cite{ernst2006-virtual-class-calculus}
formalized family polymorphism in the virtual class calculus, where
classes are accessed via path expressions \texttt{this.out.C} and the
linearization lets the rightmost operand win, so composition is not
commutative.
Amin et al.~\cite{amin2016-dependent-object-types} formalized path-dependent types
in the DOT calculus, the theoretical foundation of Scala.
On resolving an enclosing class along every route, J\&~\cite{nystrom2006-nested-intersection} comes
closest: building on the prefix types of Jx~\cite{nystrom2004-nested-inheritance}, which name the
enclosing class of a nested class inherited into a new container, it composes families by nested
intersection, so that a class extending classes nested in several containers has the intersection of
those containers as its prefix.\footnote{The formal definition of the prefix in its technical
  report~\cite[Figure~12]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection-report} relates two containers only when both
  further bind one nested class of a third, so for a name written in a common superclass of the
  containers it gives that superclass alone, contrary to the property the report states for
  it~\cite[Appendix~A.3]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection-report}; its released compiler instead
  collects every container that is a subtype of the superclass.}
Its composition is not deep merge: nested classes that share a name are intersected only when a common
ancestor introduced the name, and an intersection in which two methods override one method is abstract
until a subclass overrides it~\cite[\S~3.3]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection}.
% 作者侧（去重,2026-10-24,已批准的计划 A）:删去"computes the classes ahead of time",第 2 节"nested intersection computes the
%   nested classes ahead of time"一句已说。"so its nested classes alone cannot compute in general"一度随之删去,用户:"我觉得不够。
%   需要明确说J&的无方法子语言不能做通用计算",原样恢复(用户 2026-10-20 也要这一实现限制在正文)。
Its implementation admits neither cyclic inheritance nor a nested class that is a subtype of its
container, so its nested classes alone cannot compute in general; computation stays in method bodies, whose dispatch its formal semantics leaves unspecified when one
method overrides another~\cite[Appendix~A]{nystrom2006-nested-intersection-report}, and at run time a
reference denotes a single object.
% 作者侧（J&）:用户作者注"不用multi-target this就意味着必须要在内层循环动态生成类。也就是说J&没有给出一个高效的运行时操作语义,其小步语义依赖所有类都提前生成。"
%   用户 2026-10-20:"在脚注里写出具体它们的语义纰漏。不支持循环不能在无方法子语言做通用计算作为正文出现的实现限制。";"我是说在multi-target this
%   这一点最接近。在deep merge方面不接近";"J&已经十分接近我们想要的multi-target this,但是它们印的论文有一处纰漏,实现没照着错误的语义实现,但是实现引入了别的bug和限制"。
%   脚注的依据:技术报告 Figure 12 的 REL-FB 与 prefix;编译器 jxmi/types/JxMITypeSystem_c.java 的 computePrefix/prePrefix 用 isSubtype(index, bound)。
In gbeta, the virtual class calculus, and DOT, each path resolves to a
\emph{single} class in a \emph{single} enclosing object;
none provides set-valued resolution, and Scala rejects
the multi-target situation at compile time
(Appendix~\ref{app:scala-multi-target}).
\fi

\paragraph{\bilingual{Merge operators and nested composition}{合并算子与嵌套组合}}
% 作者侧(2026-10-08):NeColus、CP、DeepFJig 是嵌套组合的合并算子,与第 8 节「the only one we found」
%   一句相关;该句不变,因为每篇都拒绝或排序重叠定义。事实取自 inheritance-calculus-prior-work-english.md
%   的 NeColus、CP、DeepFJig 条目(§2.3 p.22:9 两句原话,Cor. 21/22 的前提,CP 的 top-like 谓词,
%   DeepFJig 的「at most one is non abstract」)。只复述对方,止于其局限。
\ifPaperChinese
Bi、Oliveira 与 Schrijvers~\cite{bi2018-nested-composition} 在 NeColus 中为不相交交类型加入了嵌套组合:合并在类型不相交的记录中递归,否则不良类型,因此“\texttt{1 ,{}, 1} is not expressible”,两个都包含同一个 \texttt{implLang} 的扩展无法组合,而其交换律与结合律对良类型的合并成立。组合式编程~\cite{zhang2021-compositional-programming} 建立在同样的不相交性之上,因此一个提供了任何东西的 trait 无法与自身合并,而它用于消解冲突的算子是有偏的。DeepFJig~\cite{corradi2012-deepfjig} 递归且对称地对嵌套类求和,但两个同名成员中至多一个可以是具体的,因此一个带具体成员的类无法与自身求和。按第~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}~节的分类,三者都在重叠时取冲突拒绝;NeColus 早期绑定 self,而组合式编程与 DeepFJig 把它晚绑定到单一目标。
\else
Bi, Oliveira, and Schrijvers~\cite{bi2018-nested-composition} add
nested composition to disjoint intersection types in NeColus: a merge
recurses through records whose types are disjoint and is ill-typed
otherwise, so ``\texttt{1 ,{}, 1} is not expressible'', two extensions
that both contain the same \texttt{implLang} cannot be composed, and
commutativity and associativity hold for merges that are well typed.
Compositional Programming~\cite{zhang2021-compositional-programming}
builds on the same disjointness, so a trait that provides anything
cannot be merged with itself, and its operators for resolving a
conflict are biased.
DeepFJig~\cite{corradi2012-deepfjig} sums nested classes recursively
and symmetrically, but at most one of two same-named members may be
concrete, so a class with a concrete member cannot be summed with
itself.
In the taxonomy of Section~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}, all three take conflict rejection on an
overlap; NeColus binds self early, while Compositional Programming and DeepFJig bind it late to a
single target.
\fi

%   CUE、Jsonnet 的定律与"不禁止递归会使 CUE 图灵完备"、NixOS 模块的深度合并已在第 2 节与表 1,NixOS 拒绝多目标情形已在第 2.6 节。
%   Dhall 只在此段被引,随段删去。两段删后本小节只剩模块演算一段,小节名改为该段的名字,段名去掉。原文见提交说明。
\subsection{\bilingual{Module Calculi and Feature Composition}{模块演算与特征组合}}

% 作者侧(2026-10-08):CMS 与 Newspeak 与摘要的统一主张相关,该主张不变:CMS 是叠在核心演算上的
%   模块层,Newspeak 的 slot、method、嵌套类仍是三种声明;gDeep 有序。事实取自 inheritance-calculus-prior-work-english.md 与 cheatsheet
%   的对应条目。
% 作者侧（去重,2026-10-24,已批准的计划 A）:删去 Jigsaw 一句与 Apel 等人特征代数一句,二者已在第 2 节与表 1;分类句随之去掉
%   二者。原文与两者的出处说明见提交说明。
\ifPaperChinese
Ancona 与 Zucca 的 CMS~\cite{ancona2002-module-systems-calculus} 是一个建立在任意核心演算之上的模块演算,并把 $\lambda$-演算与 Abadi--Cardelli 对象演算编码进其中;它的和拒绝两个模块都导出的名字,且并是扁平的。在 Newspeak~\cite{bracha2010-newspeak-modules-as-objects} 中,顶层类定义模块,每个类都是 mixin;slot、方法与嵌套类可以彼此覆写,但仍是三种声明形式,而覆写整体替换一个类。Apel 与 Hutchins 的 gDeep~\cite{apel2010-uniform-feature-composition} 以有序的函数复合跨制品统一地组合特征,其中后来的特征精化先前的特征,而未标注的名字冲突被拒绝。按第~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}~节的分类,CMS 的和与 gDeep 未标注的冲突取冲突拒绝,Newspeak 的覆写与 gDeep 标注的覆写取优先级合并;CMS 早期绑定其引用。
\else
Ancona and Zucca's CMS~\cite{ancona2002-module-systems-calculus} is
a module calculus over an arbitrary core calculus, into which it
encodes the $\lambda$-calculus and the Abadi--Cardelli object
calculus; its sum rejects a name that both modules export, and the
union is flat.
In Newspeak~\cite{bracha2010-newspeak-modules-as-objects}, a top-level
class defines a module and every class is a mixin; slots, methods, and
nested classes override one another but remain three forms of
declaration, and overriding replaces a class whole.
Apel and Hutchins's gDeep~\cite{apel2010-uniform-feature-composition}
composes the features of any artifact uniformly by ordered function
composition, in which a later feature refines an earlier one and an
unmarked name clash is rejected.
In the taxonomy of Section~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}, the sum of CMS and the unmarked clashes of
gDeep take conflict rejection, while the overriding of Newspeak and the marked overrides of gDeep take
priority merge; CMS binds its references early.
\fi

\label{sec:conclusion}

%   测试结果";"只把yield改agree，不能加and of an encoding of pure Datalog，也不能加in the test，也不能加任何别的"。故只把引言与本句的 yields
%   换成 agrees with;代理先前加的 "the answers of"、"in our tests"、"and of an encoding of pure Datalog",以及对第 7 节与 Datalog 附录(原文是 produce,
%   不是 yield)的改动,都已恢复原文。
\ifPaperChinese
第~\ref{sec:introduction}~节问了一门语言能否既是通用的,又由构造保证模块化。继承演算回答说能,凭借一种构造,即深可合并 mixin;它统一了面向对象语言与配置语言的构造。在通用一侧,它充分地嵌入惰性 $\lambda$-演算,并按 Felleisen 的意义比它更具宏表达力(第~\ref{sec:translation}~节),而其中的普通算术符合逻辑编程的关系语义(第~\ref{sec:case-study}~节与第~\ref{sec:emergent-phenomena}~节)。在模块化一侧,第~\ref{sec:modularity}~节证明它由构造满足 Meyer 的全部五条标准,其中可理解性读作局部性。在第~\ref{sec:prior-conflicts}~节所回顾的标准之间的冲突中,它解决了两个。一个 mixin 在广度上与深度上一直拆到它的叶,而每个部分都可以放到别处,所以可分解性与可组合性一致。先前的设计由构造满足其中一些标准,但把它们合在一起,会沿多条继承路线带入同一个嵌套作用域,而只有一个目标的晚绑定引用在那里必须拒绝该组合或选择一条路线(第~\ref{sec:prior-modularity}~节与第~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}~节);multi-target $\this$ 沿每条路线解析该引用。可分解性与信息隐藏之间的冲突仍然存在:信息隐藏可以在语言的一个变种中、以可分解性为代价加入。

继承演算保留了面向对象语言的多重继承,只替换了其方法层面的遮蔽:$\overrides$ 方程~(\ref{eq:overrides}) 把子类中重新定义的方法与被继承的方法深合并,而不是遮蔽后者,因此覆写一个方法本身就是多重继承。在这个意义上,继承演算是更纯粹的面向对象。面向对象编程传统上被归类为命令式的;去掉可变状态和函数体,便只剩成员定义与继承,这就是第~\ref{sec:introduction-approach}~节意义上的纯声明式编程。

单个 mixin 同时是数据、程序、配置、函数、关系和对象。我们提出继承演算作为通用编程的基础。
\else
Section~\ref{sec:introduction} asked whether a language can be both
general-purpose and modular by construction. Inheritance-calculus answers
that it can, with one construct, the deep-mergeable mixin, which unifies the
constructs of object-oriented and configuration languages. Toward generality, it embeds the lazy
$\lambda$-calculus adequately and is more macro-expressive than it in
Felleisen's sense (Section~\ref{sec:translation}), and ordinary arithmetic
in it agrees with the relational semantics of logic programming
(Sections~\ref{sec:case-study} and~\ref{sec:emergent-phenomena}). Toward
modularity, Section~\ref{sec:modularity} proves that it meets all five of
Meyer's criteria by construction, with understandability read as locality.
Of the conflicts among the criteria that Section~\ref{sec:prior-conflicts} recalls, it resolves two.
A mixin splits in breadth and in depth down to its leaves, and each part can be placed elsewhere, so
decomposability and composability agree.
Prior designs meet some of the criteria by construction, but combining them brings one nested scope in
along several inheritance routes, where a late-bound reference with a single target must reject the
composition or select one route (Sections~\ref{sec:prior-modularity}
and~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}); multi-target $\this$ resolves the reference along every route.
The conflict between decomposability and information hiding remains: information hiding can be added at
the cost of decomposability, in a variant of the language.

% 作者侧(2026-10-09):这句为下一句「纯声明式编程」作依据:读者的面向对象直觉可以照搬,新东西在
%   overrides 方程。用户:"你对动机的理解是对的,但是我不同意改法。我认为此处应该避免用技术术语,避免claim即可,
%   此处应最小化改动但同时避免claim"。故只陈述 overrides 替换了什么:不写 minimal(最小性)、single(唯一性)、
%   non-conservative extension(需要固定的基理论)、standard semantics(论文没有这个基准)。
% 作者侧(用户 2026-10-17):原句"没错,但是缺少了对比。至少我们需要非正式的说我们保持了面向风格的多重继承,
%   只是替换了……,因此方法层面的override也变成了多重继承。从这个角度看,这是更纯粹的面向对象。"故补上对比,
%   仍是非正式说法,不引入新术语。
% 作者侧(用户 2026-10-19):"纯意味着移除其他所有构造，比如方法，这就和结论呼应了";"纯声明其实是成员定义加继承。这在配置
%   语言中和OO都是标配。"原先这里的"纯面向对象编程"连同解释"纯"两层含义的脚注,改为指回 §1.2 对纯声明式编程的定义;
%   "a minimal computational model" 一并删去,它是最小性的 claim,与上一条作者注的约束一致(代理决定)。
Inheritance-calculus keeps the multiple inheritance of
object-oriented languages and replaces only their method-level
shadowing: the $\overrides$ equation~(\ref{eq:overrides})
deep-merges a method redefined in a subclass with the inherited
method instead of shadowing it, so overriding a method is itself
multiple inheritance.
In this sense, inheritance-calculus is more purely object-oriented.
Object-oriented programming is traditionally classified as
imperative; removing mutable state and function bodies leaves
member definitions and inheritance, which is pure declarative
programming in the sense of Section~\ref{sec:introduction-approach}.

A single mixin is at once data, program, configuration, function, relation,
and object. We propose inheritance-calculus as a foundation for
general-purpose programming.
\fi
