\begin{proof}[\bilingual{Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:decomposition}}{定理~\ref{thm:decomposition} 的证明}]
\ifPaperChinese
\begin{sloppypar}
我们先证 (a),再分别证明 (b) 的两个方向;两个方向都已在 Lean~4 中机械化(表~\ref{tab:lean-correspondence})。(a):$p$ 写下,故 $\init(p)$ 写下;$\init(p) \snoc R$ 与每个 $q_i \cat v$ 的前缀都写下,因为 $W_i$ 前缀封闭;部分的引用由定义给出。

对 (b),记 $\bar r = \init(p) \snoc R$,令 $A$ 为不出现 $R$ 的路径,连同 $\bar r$ 与以某个 $q_i$ 为前缀的路径所成的集合。$\varphi$ 在 $A$ 上有定义,与追加不出现 $R$ 的后缀交换,并在 $A \setminus \{\bar r\}$ 上与 $\init$ 交换。先立三个基本事实。(K1) $\supers_{D'}(\bar r) = \{(\init(p), \bar r)\}$:以 $R$ 结尾的写下路径只有 $\bar r$,故 $\overrides_{D'}(\bar r) = \{\bar r\}$,而 $\inherits_{D'}(\bar r) = \varnothing$ 给出 $\bases_{D'}(\bar r) = \varnothing$。(K2) 对 $b \in A$,$q_i \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$ 蕴含 $b = q_i$:否则 $\bar r$ 是 $\supers_{D'}(\init(b))$ 的一个 override 分量,这要求 $\init(b)$ 以 $R$ 结尾,于是 $\init(b) = \bar r$,$b = q_i$。(K3) 写在 $q_i \cat v$ 的引用,其 $\this_{D'}$ 爬升所访问的定义站点 $\init(q_i \cat v), \init^2(q_i \cat v), \ldots$ 在第 $|v| + 1$ 个处是 $\bar r$,所以爬升经过 $\bar r$ 当且仅当其索引超过 $|v|$,由平移这恰当该引用离开 $M$ 时成立;且爬升从不停在 $\bar r$,那需要索引恰为 $|v|$,平移排除了这一点。$D$ 中的引用不指名 $R$,而 $r_i = (|\init(o)|, q_i)$ 是绝对的,故从 $A$ 出发的 $\bases^*_{D'}$ 链停留在 $A$ 中。

对 $D'$ 中的 Kleene 秩作强归纳(每次降秩都来自末步反演,引理~\ref{lem:kleene-iteration}),对 $a \in A \setminus \{\bar r\}$ 同时证明四条。
(B1) $(s, o) \in \supers_{D'}(a)$ 蕴含 $(\varphi(s), \varphi(o)) \in \supers_D(\varphi(a))$。
(B2) $o \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$ 蕴含 $\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(a))$。
(B3) $t \in \bases_{D'}(a)$ 蕴含 $t \in A \setminus \{\bar r\}$ 且 $\varphi(t) \in \bases_D(\varphi(a))$;从而从 $a$ 出发的 $\bases^*_{D'}$ 链映为从 $\varphi(a)$ 出发的 $\bases^*_D$ 链。
(B4) 对 $s \in A$ 与写下的 $d \in A$,$t \in \this_{D'}(\{s\}, d, n')$ 蕴含 $\varphi(t) \in \this_D(\{\varphi(s)\}, \varphi(d), n)$,其中当 $\bar r$ 在爬升访问的定义站点 $d, \init(d), \ldots, \init^{n'-1}(d)$ 之中时 $n = n' - 1$,否则 $n = n'$。

(B1):若 $a = ()$,两边都是 $\{((), ())\}$。否则反演给出一条链 $a \to^* b$ 与 $o \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$,$s = \init(b)$;由 (B3),$b \in A \setminus \{\bar r\}$ 且 $\varphi(a) \to^* \varphi(b)$;由 (B2),$\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(b))$;而 $\varphi(s) = \init(\varphi(b))$。

(B2):若 $a = ()$,则 $o = ()$。设 $a = a_0 \snoc \ell$。若 $a_0 = \bar r$,则 $a = q_i$,由 (K1) 唯一的分支是 $\bar r$,故 $o = q_i$,而 $\varphi(o) = p \in \overrides_D(p)$。否则反演给出分支 $(\cdot, b) \in \supers_{D'}(a_0)$,$b \snoc \ell$ 写下,$o \in \{a, b \snoc \ell\}$;$b \ne \init(p)$ 或 $\ell \ne R$,因为 $a \ne \bar r$;由 (B1),$\varphi(b)$ 是 $\supers_D(\varphi(a_0))$ 的分支,且 $\varphi(b \snoc \ell) = \varphi(b) \snoc \ell$ 在 $D$ 中写下;故 $\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(a))$。

(B3):反演给出 $o \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$,携带 $(n', w) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$,以及 $c \in \this_{D'}(\{a_0\}, \init(o), n')$,$t = c \cat w$。由 (B2),$\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(a))$。若 $o$ 不出现 $R$,且该引用不是某个 $r_i$,它也写在 $D$ 中的 $o$ 处;$\bar r$ 不在其爬升访问的站点之中,由 (B4),$\varphi(c) \in \this_D(\{\varphi(a_0)\}, \init(o), n')$,故 $\varphi(t) = \varphi(c) \cat w \in \bases_D(\varphi(a))$。若该引用是 $r_i$,它是绝对的,如引理~\ref{lem:redirection-simulation} 的证明,爬升终于根,$c = ()$,$t = q_i$;由 (B4),$() \in \this_D(\{\varphi(a_0)\}, \init(o), n')$,故 $D$ 中 $o$ 处的 $r$ 给出 $p = \varphi(t)$。若 $o = q_i \cat v$,则该引用为 $\mathit{shift}_v(\rho)$,$\rho = (n, w) \in \inherits_D(p \cat v)$;由 (K3) 与 (B4),$\varphi(c) \in \this_D(\{\varphi(a_0)\}, \init(p \cat v), n)$,故 $\varphi(t) \in \bases_D(\varphi(a))$,由 $p \cat v = \varphi(o)$ 处的 $\rho$ 给出。$o = \bar r$ 不可能,因为 $a \ne \bar r$。$t \in A$ 已在上面说明;$t \ne \bar r$ 由 (K3) 与 $r_i$ 的形状得出。链的情形对长度归纳。

(B4):$n' = 0$ 的情形是直接的。对 $n' > 0$,反演给出 $(s_1, d) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$ 与 $t \in \this_{D'}(\{s_1\}, \init(d), n' - 1)$,两者秩更小。若 $d = \bar r$:该对来自一条链 $s \to^* b$,$\bar r \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$,这迫使 $b$ 以 $R$ 结尾,故 $b = \bar r$;而由 (B3),从 $s \ne \bar r$ 出发的链不终于 $\bar r$,所以 $s = \bar r$;由 (K1),$s_1 = \init(p) = \varphi(s)$,且 $\init(d) = \init(p) = \varphi(d)$;归纳假设给出余下的爬升,这一步在 $D$ 中没有对应,恰好扣去 $n$ 中的 $1$。若 $d \ne \bar r$,则 $s \ne \bar r$(否则由 (K1),$d = \bar r$);由 (B1),$(\varphi(s_1), \varphi(d)) \in \supers_D(\varphi(s))$,这是 $D$ 中的一步;$\varphi(\init(d)) = \init(\varphi(d))$,归纳假设覆盖余下的 $n' - 1$ 步。

对不出现 $R$ 的 $a$,$\varphi(a) = a$,由 (B1),$\supers_{D'}(a)$ 的每个书写分量的像都是 $\supers_D(a)$ 的书写分量;特别地,$a$ 在 $D'$ 中存在蕴含它在 $D$ 中存在。

由 $D$ 到 $D'$ 的包含。下面的路径都不出现 $R$。对 $D$ 中的 Kleene 秩作强归纳,同时证明四条。
(F1) 对 $(s_1, e) \in \supers_D(s)$:若 $e$ 不在 $p$ 的子树中,则 $(s_1, e) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$;若 $e = p \cat y$,则或者 $(s_1, e) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$,或者对每个满足 $y \in W_i$ 的 $i$,都有 $(s'_1, q_i \cat y) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$,$\varphi(s'_1) = s_1$。
(F2) 对 $e \in \overrides_D(b)$:若 $e$ 不在 $p$ 的子树中,或 $e = p$,则 $e \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$;若 $e = p \cat y$ 且 $y \ne ()$,则或者 $e \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$,或者对每个满足 $y \in W_i$ 的 $i$,都有 $q_i \cat y \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$。
(F3) $D$ 中一条链 $x \to^* t$ 给出 $D'$ 中一条链 $x \to^* t$,或者 $t = p$ 且对每个 $i$ 都有 $D'$ 中的链 $x \to^* q_i$。
(F4) 对 $p$ 的子树中一条写下的路径 $d = p \cat y_0$ 与 $t \in \this_D(\{s_0\}, d, n)$:或者 $t \in \this_{D'}(\{s_0\}, d, n)$,或者对每个满足 $y_0 \in W_i$ 的 $i$,都有 $t \in \this_{D'}(\{s_0\}, q_i \cat y_0, n')$,其中当爬升访问 $p$,即 $n > |y_0|$ 时 $n' = n + 1$,否则 $n' = n$。

(F1):若 $s = ()$ 则是直接的。否则反演给出一条链 $s \to^* b$ 与 $e \in \overrides_D(b)$,$s_1 = \init(b)$。若由 (F3) 链在 $D'$ 中不变,(F2) 给出结论,站点仍是 $s_1$。否则 $b = p$,而 $\overrides_D(p) = \{p\}$ 给出 $e = p$;每条链 $s \to^* q_i$ 与 $\overrides_{D'}(q_i) = \{q_i\}$(由 (K1))给出 $(\bar r, q_i) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$,而 $\varphi(\bar r) = \init(p) = s_1$。

(F2):若 $b = ()$ 则 $e = ()$。设 $b = b_0 \snoc \ell$;反演给出分支 $(\cdot, e_0) \in \supers_D(b_0)$,$e_0 \snoc \ell$ 写下,$e \in \{b, e_0 \snoc \ell\}$。对这个分支用 (F1)。若它在 $D'$ 中不变,$e$ 的两种取法都在 $D'$ 中产生。否则 $e_0 = p \cat y$,对每个满足 $y \in W_i$ 的 $i$,$q_i \cat y$ 是 $\supers_{D'}(b_0)$ 的分支。由覆盖,$p \cat y \snoc \ell$ 是某个 $p \cat v$,$v \in W_j$;于是 $y \in W_j$,$q_j \cat y \snoc \ell$ 写下,故自反的 $b$ 在 $\overrides_{D'}(b)$ 中;而对每个满足 $y \snoc \ell \in W_i$ 的 $i$,$q_i \cat y \snoc \ell \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$。在这种情形下 $e_0$ 在子树中,所以 $e_0 \snoc \ell \ne p$,产生的 override 只有自反的 $b$ 与后缀非空的 $p \cat y \snoc \ell$,两者上面都已处理。

(F3):先看一跳 $t \in \bases_D(x)$;反演给出携带 $\rho = (n, w) \in \inherits_D(o)$ 的 $o \in \overrides_D(x)$,以及一次爬升 $c \in \this_D(\{\init(x)\}, \init(o), n)$,$t = c \cat w$。若 $o$ 不在子树中,或 $o = p$,则由 (F2) $o \in \overrides_{D'}(o)$ 的对应成立,即 $o \in \overrides_{D'}(x)$;爬升访问的定义站点都不在子树中,由 (F1) 每一步在 $D'$ 中不变。若 $\rho \ne r$,$\rho \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$,给出 $t \in \bases_{D'}(x)$;若 $\rho = r$,则 $c = ()$,$t = p$,而 $(|\init(o)|, q_i)$ 给出每个 $q_i \in \bases_{D'}(x)$。若 $o = p \cat v$,$v \ne ()$:由覆盖,设 $i$ 使 $(p \cat v, \rho) \in E_i$,于是 $v \in W_i$。$n = 0$ 时用 (F2):$o$ 或 $q_i \cat v$ 在 $\overrides_{D'}(x)$ 中,分别携带 $\rho$ 或 $\mathit{shift}_v(\rho) = \rho$,给出同一个 $t = \init(x) \cat w$。$n > 0$ 时用 (F4),$y_0 = \init(v)$:第一种情形的第一步说明 $p \cat \init(v)$ 是 $\supers_{D'}(\init(x))$ 的分支,故 $o \in \overrides_{D'}(x)$(或 $x = o$),由 $\rho$ 给出 $t$;第二种情形同理给出 $q_i \cat v \in \overrides_{D'}(x)$,其引用 $\mathit{shift}_v(\rho)$ 的索引恰为 $n'$,因为 $n \ge |v|$ 当且仅当 $n > |y_0|$,给出同一个 $t$。链对长度归纳:若前一段止于 $p$ 并对应每个 $q_i$,由 $\overrides_D(p) = \{p\}$,下一跳由某个 $\rho \in \inherits_D(p)$ 给出,$(p, \rho) \in E_j$;则在 $D'$ 中从 $q_j$ 出发,它是自己唯一的 override(由 (K1)),经 $\mathit{shift}_{()}(\rho)$:其爬升先经 (K1) 从 $\bar r$ 到 $\init(p)$,其后与 $D$ 中的爬升逐步相同,给出同一个目标。

(F4):反演给出站点 $s_0, \ldots, s_n$,第 $j$ 步来自一条链 $s_j \to^* b_j$,其中 $d_j = \init^j(d) \in \overrides_D(b_j)$,$s_{j+1} = \init(b_j)$,各事实秩更小。令 $m = |y_0| + 1$,对 $j < m$,$d_j = p \cat y_j$,$d_{m-1} = p$。先定最上面一层。若 $n \ge m$:落到 $p$ 的那一步,由 (F3),链 $s_{m-1} \to^* b_{m-1}$ 或者在 $D'$ 中不变,此时由 (F2) $p \in \overrides_{D'}(b_{m-1})$,取原件版本;或者 $b_{m-1} = p$ 且有链 $s_{m-1} \to^* q_i$,于是 $(\bar r, q_i) \in \supers_{D'}(s_{m-1})$,由 (K1) 再走一步到 $\init(p) = s_m$,取部分版本;$j \ge m$ 的各步站点不在子树中,由 (F1) 不变。若 $n < m$:由最后一步的反演,$p \cat y_n$ 或者是 $\supers_D(s_n)$ 的分支(秩更小,用 (F1)),或者 $b_{n-1} = d_{n-1}$ 且 $s_n = p \cat y_n$ 本身写下(对长度归纳,$\dom(\inherits)$ 前缀封闭的文档中每条写下的路径都是自己的 override);于是 $s_n$ 在 $D'$ 中承载 $p \cat y_n$(原件版本),或对每个满足 $y_n \in W_i$ 的 $i$ 承载 $q_i \cat y_n$(部分版本)。再逐层向下:设所选版本的 $y_{j+1}$ 处的作用域是 $\supers_{D'}(s_{j+1})$ 的 override 分量。由 $\overrides_D(p) = \{p\}$,$b_j \ne p$,故由 (F3) 链 $s_j \to^* b_j$ 在 $D'$ 中不变;$b_j$ 与 $d_j$ 的末标签相同,而所选版本 $y_j$ 处的作用域写下($y_j$ 是 $y_0$ 的前缀,$W_i$ 前缀封闭),由 overrides 方程的分支条款,它属于 $\overrides_{D'}(b_j)$,故 $(s_{j+1}, \cdot) \in \supers_{D'}(s_j)$ 给出这一步。$n = 0$ 的情形是直接的。拼起来即得结论。

对不出现 $R$ 的路径 $a$,由 (F1),$\supers_D(a)$ 的每个书写分量 $e$ 或者本身是 $\supers_{D'}(a)$ 的书写分量,或者是某个书写分量 $q_i \cat y$ 的像,由覆盖,这样的 $i$ 存在;特别地,$a$ 在 $D$ 中存在蕴含它在 $D'$ 中存在。
\end{sloppypar}
\else
\begin{sloppypar}
We prove (a), and then the two inclusions of (b); both inclusions are mechanized in Lean~4
(Table~\ref{tab:lean-correspondence}). (a): $p$ is written, so $\init(p)$ is
written; every prefix of $\init(p) \snoc R$ and of each $q_i \cat v$ is written, as $W_i$ is
prefix-closed; the references of the parts are as defined.

For (b), write $\bar r = \init(p) \snoc R$, and let $A$ be the set of paths in which $R$ does not occur,
together with $\bar r$ and the paths with a prefix $q_i$. The map $\varphi$ is defined on $A$, commutes
with appending a suffix in which $R$ does not occur, and commutes with $\init$ on
$A \setminus \{\bar r\}$. Three ground facts first. (K1) $\supers_{D'}(\bar r) = \{(\init(p), \bar r)\}$:
the only written path ending in $R$ is $\bar r$, so $\overrides_{D'}(\bar r) = \{\bar r\}$, and
$\inherits_{D'}(\bar r) = \varnothing$ gives $\bases_{D'}(\bar r) = \varnothing$. (K2) For $b \in A$,
$q_i \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$ implies $b = q_i$: otherwise $\bar r$ is an override component of
$\supers_{D'}(\init(b))$, which needs $\init(b)$ to end in $R$, so $\init(b) = \bar r$ and $b = q_i$.
(K3) For a reference written at $q_i \cat v$, the definition sites
$\init(q_i \cat v), \init^2(q_i \cat v), \ldots$ that its climb in $\this_{D'}$ visits reach $\bar r$ at
the $(|v| + 1)$-st, so the climb passes $\bar r$ exactly when its index exceeds $|v|$, which by the
shift holds exactly when the reference leaves $M$; and the climb never ends at $\bar r$, which would
need the index to be $|v|$, as the shift excludes. The references of $D$ name no path through $R$, and
each $r_i = (|\init(o)|, q_i)$ is absolute, so a $\bases^*_{D'}$ chain from a path of $A$ stays in $A$.

By strong induction on the Kleene rank in $D'$ (every rank decrease from last-step inversion,
Lemma~\ref{lem:kleene-iteration}), we prove four clauses simultaneously, for $a \in A \setminus \{\bar r\}$.
(B1) $(s, o) \in \supers_{D'}(a)$ implies $(\varphi(s), \varphi(o)) \in \supers_D(\varphi(a))$.
(B2) $o \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$ implies $\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(a))$.
(B3) $t \in \bases_{D'}(a)$ implies $t \in A \setminus \{\bar r\}$ and
$\varphi(t) \in \bases_D(\varphi(a))$; hence a $\bases^*_{D'}$ chain from $a$ maps to a $\bases^*_D$
chain from $\varphi(a)$.
(B4) For $s \in A$ and a written $d \in A$, $t \in \this_{D'}(\{s\}, d, n')$ implies
$\varphi(t) \in \this_D(\{\varphi(s)\}, \varphi(d), n)$, where $n = n' - 1$ if $\bar r$ is among the
definition sites $d, \init(d), \ldots, \init^{n'-1}(d)$ that the climb visits, and $n = n'$ otherwise.

(B1): if $a = ()$, both sides are $\{((), ())\}$. Otherwise inversion gives a chain $a \to^* b$ and
$o \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$ with $s = \init(b)$; by (B3), $b \in A \setminus \{\bar r\}$ and
$\varphi(a) \to^* \varphi(b)$; by (B2), $\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(b))$; and
$\varphi(s) = \init(\varphi(b))$.

(B2): if $a = ()$ then $o = ()$. Let $a = a_0 \snoc \ell$. If $a_0 = \bar r$, then $a = q_i$, its only
branch is $\bar r$ by (K1), so $o = q_i$, and $\varphi(o) = p \in \overrides_D(p)$. Otherwise inversion
gives a branch $(\cdot, b) \in \supers_{D'}(a_0)$ with $b \snoc \ell$ written and
$o \in \{a, b \snoc \ell\}$; $b \snoc \ell \ne \bar r$, since $a \ne \bar r$; by (B1), $\varphi(b)$ is a
branch of $\supers_D(\varphi(a_0))$, and $\varphi(b \snoc \ell) = \varphi(b) \snoc \ell$ is written in
$D$; so $\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(a))$.

(B3): inversion gives $o \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$ carrying $(n', w) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$ and
$c \in \this_{D'}(\{a_0\}, \init(o), n')$ with $t = c \cat w$. By (B2),
$\varphi(o) \in \overrides_D(\varphi(a))$. If $R$ does not occur in $o$ and the reference is no $r_i$,
it is written at $o$ in $D$ as well; its climb visits no $\bar r$, so by (B4)
$\varphi(c) \in \this_D(\{\varphi(a_0)\}, \init(o), n')$, and
$\varphi(t) = \varphi(c) \cat w \in \bases_D(\varphi(a))$. If the reference is $r_i$, it is absolute, so,
as in the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:redirection-simulation}, its climb ends at the root, $c = ()$, and
$t = q_i$; by (B4), $() \in \this_D(\{\varphi(a_0)\}, \init(o), n')$, so the reference $r$ at $o$ in $D$
gives $p = \varphi(t)$. If $o = q_i \cat v$, the reference is $\mathit{shift}_v(\rho)$ with
$\rho = (n, w) \in \inherits_D(p \cat v)$; by (K3) and (B4),
$\varphi(c) \in \this_D(\{\varphi(a_0)\}, \init(p \cat v), n)$, so $\varphi(t) \in \bases_D(\varphi(a))$,
given by $\rho$ at $p \cat v = \varphi(o)$. The case $o = \bar r$ is impossible, as $a \ne \bar r$. That
$t \in A$ was shown above, and $t \ne \bar r$ follows from (K3) and the shape of the $r_i$. Chains
follow by induction on their length.

(B4): the case $n' = 0$ is immediate. For $n' > 0$, inversion gives $(s_1, d) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$ and
$t \in \this_{D'}(\{s_1\}, \init(d), n' - 1)$, both of smaller rank. If $d = \bar r$: the pair comes from a chain
$s \to^* b$ with $\bar r \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$, which forces $b$ to end in $R$, so $b = \bar r$, and since by (B3) no chain from $s \ne \bar r$ ends at
$\bar r$, $s = \bar r$; by (K1),
$s_1 = \init(p) = \varphi(s)$, and $\init(d) = \init(p) = \varphi(d)$; the induction hypothesis gives
the rest of the climb, and this step, which has no counterpart in $D$, accounts for the $1$ subtracted
from $n$. If $d \ne \bar r$, then $s \ne \bar r$, since by (K1) $d$ would be $\bar r$; by (B1),
$(\varphi(s_1), \varphi(d)) \in \supers_D(\varphi(s))$, a step in $D$; and
$\varphi(\init(d)) = \init(\varphi(d))$, so the induction hypothesis covers the remaining $n' - 1$
steps.

For a path $a$ in which $R$ does not occur, $\varphi(a) = a$, and by (B1) the image of every written
override component of $\supers_{D'}(a)$ is a written override component of $\supers_D(a)$; in
particular, if $a$ exists in $D'$, it exists in $D$.

The inclusion from $D$ to $D'$. All paths below are free of $R$. By strong induction on the Kleene rank in
$D$, we prove four clauses simultaneously.
(F1) For $(s_1, e) \in \supers_D(s)$: if $e$ is not in the subtree of $p$, then
$(s_1, e) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$; if $e = p \cat y$, then either $(s_1, e) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$, or for every
$i$ with $y \in W_i$ there is $(s'_1, q_i \cat y) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$ with $\varphi(s'_1) = s_1$.
(F2) For $e \in \overrides_D(b)$: if $e$ is not in the subtree of $p$, or $e = p$, then
$e \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$; if $e = p \cat y$ with $y \ne ()$, then either $e \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$, or
$q_i \cat y \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$ for every $i$ with $y \in W_i$.
(F3) A chain $x \to^* t$ in $D$ gives a chain $x \to^* t$ in $D'$, or else $t = p$ and there are chains
$x \to^* q_i$ in $D'$ for every $i$.
(F4) For a written path $d = p \cat y_0$ in the subtree of $p$ and $t \in \this_D(\{s_0\}, d, n)$: either
$t \in \this_{D'}(\{s_0\}, d, n)$, or $t \in \this_{D'}(\{s_0\}, q_i \cat y_0, n')$ for every $i$ with
$y_0 \in W_i$, where $n' = n + 1$ if the climb visits $p$, that is, if $n > |y_0|$, and $n' = n$
otherwise.

(F1): the case $s = ()$ is immediate. Otherwise inversion gives a chain $s \to^* b$ and
$e \in \overrides_D(b)$ with $s_1 = \init(b)$. If by (F3) the chain is unchanged in $D'$, (F2) gives the
claim, with the same site $s_1$. Otherwise $b = p$, and $\overrides_D(p) = \{p\}$ gives $e = p$; each
chain $s \to^* q_i$, with $\overrides_{D'}(q_i) = \{q_i\}$ by (K1), gives
$(\bar r, q_i) \in \supers_{D'}(s)$, and $\varphi(\bar r) = \init(p) = s_1$.

(F2): if $b = ()$ then $e = ()$. Let $b = b_0 \snoc \ell$; inversion gives a branch
$(\cdot, e_0) \in \supers_D(b_0)$ with $e_0 \snoc \ell$ written and $e \in \{b, e_0 \snoc \ell\}$. Apply
(F1) to this branch. If it is unchanged in $D'$, both choices of $e$ are produced in $D'$. Otherwise
$e_0 = p \cat y$ and $q_i \cat y$ is a branch of $\supers_{D'}(b_0)$ for every $i$ with $y \in W_i$. By
the cover, $p \cat y \snoc \ell$ is $p \cat v$ for some $v \in W_j$; so $y \in W_j$ and
$q_j \cat y \snoc \ell$ is written, which puts the reflexive $b$ in $\overrides_{D'}(b)$; and
$q_i \cat y \snoc \ell \in \overrides_{D'}(b)$ for every $i$ with $y \snoc \ell \in W_i$. In this case $e_0$ is in the subtree, so
$e_0 \snoc \ell \ne p$, and the only overrides produced are the reflexive $b$ and $p \cat y \snoc \ell$
with a nonempty suffix, both treated above.

(F3): consider first a hop $t \in \bases_D(x)$; inversion gives $o \in \overrides_D(x)$ carrying
$\rho = (n, w) \in \inherits_D(o)$, and a climb $c \in \this_D(\{\init(x)\}, \init(o), n)$ with
$t = c \cat w$. If $o$ is not in the subtree, or $o = p$, then $o \in \overrides_{D'}(x)$ by (F2), and the
definition sites that the climb visits are outside the subtree, so by (F1) each step is unchanged in
$D'$. If $\rho \ne r$, then $\rho \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$ gives $t \in \bases_{D'}(x)$; if $\rho = r$, then
$c = ()$ and $t = p$, and the references $(|\init(o)|, q_i)$ give $q_i \in \bases_{D'}(x)$ for every $i$.
If $o = p \cat v$ with $v \ne ()$, let $i$ be such that $(p \cat v, \rho) \in E_i$, by the cover, so
that $v \in W_i$. If $n = 0$, (F2) puts $o$ or $q_i \cat v$ in $\overrides_{D'}(x)$, carrying
$\rho$ or $\mathit{shift}_v(\rho) = \rho$ respectively, and either gives the same $t = \init(x) \cat w$. If
$n > 0$, apply (F4) with $y_0 = \init(v)$: in its first case, the first step shows that $p \cat \init(v)$ is
a branch of $\supers_{D'}(\init(x))$, so $o \in \overrides_{D'}(x)$ (or $x = o$), and $\rho$ gives $t$; in
its second case, the same argument gives $q_i \cat v \in \overrides_{D'}(x)$, whose reference
$\mathit{shift}_v(\rho)$ has index exactly $n'$, since $n \ge |v|$ exactly when $n > |y_0|$, and it gives
the same $t$. Chains follow by induction on their length: when a prefix ends at $p$ and corresponds to
every $q_i$, the next hop is given, as $\overrides_D(p) = \{p\}$, by some $\rho \in \inherits_D(p)$
with $(p, \rho) \in E_j$; continue in $D'$ from $q_j$, its own only override by (K1), with
$\mathit{shift}_{()}(\rho)$, whose climb first steps from $\bar r$ to $\init(p)$ by (K1) and then follows
the climb in $D$ step by step, giving the same target.

(F4): inversion gives sites $s_0, \ldots, s_n$ whose $j$-th step comes from a chain $s_j \to^* b_j$ with
$d_j = \init^j(d) \in \overrides_D(b_j)$ and $s_{j+1} = \init(b_j)$, all of smaller rank. Let
$m = |y_0| + 1$, so that $d_j = p \cat y_j$ for $j < m$ and $d_{m-1} = p$. First fix the top. If
$n \ge m$, consider the step that lands on $p$: by (F3), either the chain $s_{m-1} \to^* b_{m-1}$ is
unchanged in $D'$, and then $p \in \overrides_{D'}(b_{m-1})$ by (F2), and we take the version of the
original; or $b_{m-1} = p$ with chains $s_{m-1} \to^* q_i$, and then $(\bar r, q_i) \in \supers_{D'}(s_{m-1})$
and one more step by (K1) reaches $\init(p) = s_m$, and we take the version of the parts; the steps with
$j \ge m$ visit sites outside the subtree and are unchanged by (F1). If $n < m$: by inversion of the last
step, $p \cat y_n$ is either a branch of $\supers_D(s_n)$, of smaller rank, to which (F1) applies, or
$b_{n-1} = d_{n-1}$ and $s_n = p \cat y_n$ is written, and so, by induction on its length as every written path of a document with
prefix-closed $\dom(\inherits)$, is its own override in $D'$; so $s_n$ hosts in $D'$ either $p \cat y_n$ (the version of the original) or $q_i \cat y_n$ for every
$i$ with $y_n \in W_i$ (the version of the parts). Then descend level by level: suppose the scope of the
chosen version at $y_{j+1}$ is an override component of $\supers_{D'}(s_{j+1})$. As
$\overrides_D(p) = \{p\}$, $b_j \ne p$, so by (F3) the chain $s_j \to^* b_j$ is unchanged in $D'$; $b_j$
and $d_j$ have the same last label, and the scope of the chosen version at $y_j$ is written, since $y_j$
is a prefix of $y_0$ and $W_i$ is prefix-closed; by the branch clause of the equation for $\overrides$,
it belongs to $\overrides_{D'}(b_j)$, which gives the step $(s_{j+1}, \cdot) \in \supers_{D'}(s_j)$.
The case $n = 0$ is immediate. Putting the steps together gives the claim.

For a path $a$ free of $R$, by (F1) every written override component $e$ of $\supers_D(a)$ is either a
written override component of $\supers_{D'}(a)$ or the image of one, $q_i \cat y$, where such an $i$ exists by the cover; in particular, if $a$
exists in $D$, it exists in $D'$.
\end{sloppypar}
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧（LaTeX 坑,2026-07-09 排查记录）：amsthm 的 proof 以 run-in 的 Proof. 开头,
%   若 proof 环境的第一个内容就是 run-in 的 \paragraph,acmart 的 @noskipsec/\everypar 状态
%   会错乱,此后每个 \paragraph 的 \addpenalty 都报 Improper \prevdepth + missing \item,
%   且仅在冷构建的分页下必现。修法 = 证明正文先给一句引导语再进第一个 \paragraph(两个
%   语言分支都要);结合律证明的开场句同时也起这个作用,勿删。
% 作者侧（台账 #4/X4 修法，作者批准 2026-07-02）：由构造保证的可扩展性的定义是全称的，原 item:multi-dimensional-extensibility
%   一句话带过且错误归因于 thm:cartesian-nonexpressibility（其本职是表达力分离，不是可扩展性）。见证
%   构造按定义可给出：保留字包装继承 P 的顶层名并沿 p 嵌套写定义，叶子放新鲜 __new。证明
%   自底向上有限推导（构造事实而非消费事实，无需秩归纳；不变量 = 每层的写下前缀是自身的
%   override）；「不修改 P」句法成立，P 的事实不变引 thm:merge-associative 的隔离论证。
%   探针 P9（随机文档 × 随机路径，含 P 中存在与不存在两类）先行全绿。
% 作者侧（第十二轮）:prop:extensibility-by-construction 的陈述在第 5 节(sec:modularity),此处只留证明。


\begin{proof}[\bilingual{Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:extensibility-by-construction}}{命题~\ref{prop:extensibility-by-construction} 的证明}]
\ifPaperChinese
  存在性:记 $w_j = (\text{``\_\_extend''}) \cat (\ell_1, \ldots, \ell_j)$
  ($0 \le j \le k$),每个 $w_j$ 都是写下位置(嵌套定义链)。对 $j$ 作
  平凡归纳,不变量为 $w_j \in \overrides(w_j)$。基例:分支
  $((),\, ()) \in \supers(())$(方程~\ref{eq:supers} 基例)满足守卫
  $() \snoc \text{``\_\_extend''} \in \dom(\inherits)$,方程~(\ref{eq:overrides})
  产出 $w_0 \in \overrides(w_0)$。归纳步:由 $w_j \in \overrides(w_j)$
  与 $\bases^*$ 的自反性,$(\init(w_j),\, w_j) \in \supers(w_j)$;
  守卫 $w_j \snoc \ell_{j+1} \in \dom(\inherits)$ 成立(写下),
  产出 $w_{j+1} \in \overrides(w_{j+1})$。末了,分支
  $(\_,\, w_k) \in \supers(w_k)$ 满足
  $w_k \snoc \text{``\_\_new''} \in \dom(\inherits)$,由路径存在性引理
  (引理~\ref{lem:path-existence}),
  $(\text{``\_\_extend''}) \cat p \cat (\text{``\_\_new''})$ 存在。
  归纳只自底向上构造事实、不消费事实,故无需秩。

  隔离与 (ii):$\ell_P$ 之下的文本按构造原样。保留根
  $(\text{``\_\_extend''})$ 路径长度为 1,且 \mintinline{yaml}{__extend}
  与 \mintinline{yaml}{__new} 不出现于任何引用,故固定目标
  $p_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w_{\mathrm{ref}}$ 落入保留子树只可能经
  $p_{\mathrm{current}} = ()$ 且首投影标签为保留字,被排除;保留子树外
  路径的推导从不触及该子树,其事实字面不变,(ii) 得证。

  两点收尾。其一,(i) 中后缀 $(\text{``\_\_new''})$ 是新的:\mintinline{yaml}{__new}
  不书写于 $P$ 中任何位置,而按引理~\ref{lem:path-existence},
  $(\ell_P) \cat p \snoc \text{``\_\_new''}$ 在 $P$ 中存在须有某书写分支
  定义 \mintinline{yaml}{__new}。其二,合成体良构:包装的引用在合成体中
  解析到顶层 $\ell_P$,不再爬空,其余良构性情形
  (附录~\ref{app:well-definedness})均节点局部,在写下的包装处逐一成立。
\else
  Existence: write
  $w_j = (\text{``\_\_extend''}) \cat (\ell_1, \ldots, \ell_j)$ for
  $0 \le j \le k$; every $w_j$ is a written position (the nested
  definition chain). By a plain induction on $j$ with the invariant
  $w_j \in \overrides(w_j)$. Base: the branch
  $((),\, ()) \in \supers(())$ (the base case of
  equation~\ref{eq:supers}) satisfies the guard
  $() \snoc \text{``\_\_extend''} \in \dom(\inherits)$, so
  equation~(\ref{eq:overrides}) produces $w_0 \in \overrides(w_0)$.
  Step: from $w_j \in \overrides(w_j)$ and the reflexivity of
  $\bases^*$, $(\init(w_j),\, w_j) \in \supers(w_j)$; the guard
  $w_j \snoc \ell_{j+1} \in \dom(\inherits)$ holds (written), and
  the comprehension produces $w_{j+1} \in \overrides(w_{j+1})$.
  Finally the branch $(\_,\, w_k) \in \supers(w_k)$ satisfies
  $w_k \snoc \text{``\_\_new''} \in \dom(\inherits)$, and the Path
  Existence Lemma~\ref{lem:path-existence} yields the existence of
  $(\text{``\_\_extend''}) \cat p \cat (\text{``\_\_new''})$. The
  induction only constructs facts bottom-up and consumes none, so no
  rank is needed.

  Insulation and (ii): the text under $\ell_P$ is untouched by
  construction. The reserved root $(\text{``\_\_extend''})$ has
  length one, and \mintinline{yaml}{__extend} and
  \mintinline{yaml}{__new} appear in no reference, so a fixed target
  $p_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w_{\mathrm{ref}}$ lands inside the
  reserved subtree only through $p_{\mathrm{current}} = ()$ with the
  reserved word as first projection label, which is excluded; the
  derivations of paths outside the reserved subtree never touch it,
  their facts are literally unchanged, and (ii) holds.

  Two closing points. First, the suffix $(\text{``\_\_new''})$ of (i)
  is new: \mintinline{yaml}{__new} is written nowhere in $P$, and by
  Lemma~\ref{lem:path-existence} the existence of
  $(\ell_P) \cat p \snoc \text{``\_\_new''}$ in $P$ would require
  some written branch defining \mintinline{yaml}{__new}. Second, the
  composite is well-formed: the wrapper's reference resolves to the
  top-level $\ell_P$ in the composite, no longer climbing past the
  root, and the remaining well-formedness cases
  (Appendix~\ref{app:well-definedness}) are node-local and hold at
  the written wrapper.
\fi
\end{proof}

%   并把「继承演算的继承关系具有 substitutability」写成已证(旁注 % TODO(未证):)。以下结果兑现这些 TODO。
%   定义沿用任务书给出的作者措辞:(a) T 良构则 S 良构,只计组合本身引起的错误,S 自己犯的错(如引用未定义
%   的名字)不计;(b) T 的每个观测都是 S 的观测。保证的是包含,不是「不变」。
% 作者侧（陈述选择,代理 2026-10-17,未经用户裁定）：
%   · 观测沿用第 7 节的定义(后缀集);另用更细的「书写观测」(supers 中属于 dom(inherits) 的 override 分量),
%     即 prop:extensibility-by-construction (ii) 比较的那个量。「读点 q 的观测」= supers(q) 中的对,这是第 3 节「supers 是观测语义」。
%   · 继承关系 = T ∈ bases*(S),含传递继承。
%   · 「S 良构」= S 的书写位置上不出现良构性四种情形;(a) 的形式是「位置良构与否只读该处节点与外围作用域写下的
%     标签」,于是 T 的体不可能造成 S 的错误。顶层重名(Q 写了 P 已写的顶层标签)按「S 自己犯的错」计:它与
%     「引用未定义的名字」对偶,都是名字空间错误,不涉及任何引用或继承。这一归类是代理的选择。
%   · 组合体 = 第 7 节定义的 composite,根 mixin 的成员表是 P 的根成员后接 Q 的成员(串接)。

% 作者侧：TPfun_mono 是对解释(查询-答案映射)单调,程序固定;这里要的是对程序单调。证明只依赖方程读原始量的
%   两处都是正向的(overrides 的守卫与 bases 的绑定子)。Lean:ProgramMonotonicity.lean 的 Extends.fact。
% 作者侧（边界,不入正文;代理 2026-10-17）：对【文本】不单调。词法引用的 de Bruijn 索引由 lexical 的
%   「最近定义者」爬升算出,其 otherwise 分支读的是「外围作用域没写这个标签」。在外围作用域加一条同名定义会
%   捕获该引用,inherits 不再是扩展。最小见证(已用 Lean 的认证解释器核对,证书被验证过的检查器接受):
%     b: [foo: []]          h: [y: [[b]]]         ⟹ (h, y, foo) 存在
%     再在 h 里加 b: []      ⟹ [b] 的索引由 1 变 0,解析到 (h, b),(h, y, foo) 不再存在。
%   因此本引理比较的是原始量,组合体之所以是扩展,靠的是 Q 只在顶层加新标签(lem:composite-extension)。
\begin{lemma}[\bilingual{Monotonicity in the program}{对程序的单调性}]\label{lem:program-monotonicity}
\ifPaperChinese
设 $\inherits$ 与 $\inherits'$ 是两个原始查询,满足 $\dom(\inherits) \subseteq \dom(\inherits')$,且对每个 $p \in \dom(\inherits)$ 有 $\inherits(p) \subseteq \inherits'(p)$;我们说 $\inherits'$ \emph{扩展}了 $\inherits$。则 $\inherits$ 之上 $\lfp(T_P)$ 的每个事实都是 $\inherits'$ 之上 $\lfp(T_P)$ 的事实;特别地,对每条路径 $q$,$\inherits$ 之上的 $\supers(q)$ 包含于 $\inherits'$ 之上的 $\supers(q)$,且在 $\inherits$ 之上存在的每条路径在 $\inherits'$ 之上也存在。
\else
Let $\inherits$ and $\inherits'$ be two primitive queries with
$\dom(\inherits) \subseteq \dom(\inherits')$ and $\inherits(p) \subseteq \inherits'(p)$ for every
$p \in \dom(\inherits)$; we say that $\inherits'$ \emph{extends} $\inherits$. Then every fact of
$\lfp(T_P)$ over $\inherits$ is a fact of $\lfp(T_P)$ over $\inherits'$; in particular, for every
path $q$, $\supers(q)$ over $\inherits$ is contained in $\supers(q)$ over $\inherits'$, and every path
that exists over $\inherits$ exists over $\inherits'$.
\fi
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
我们在一切路径上的五个函数的全部查询之上读取两个最小不动点;这一查询集包含由任一 AST 产生的查询,且由引理~\ref{lem:dependency-restriction},最小不动点在由一个 AST 产生的、对依赖封闭的查询集上的限制不因加入其他查询而改变。对 $\inherits$ 之上的 Kleene 秩作强归纳(引理~\ref{lem:kleene-iteration})。秩为 $m + 1$ 的事实由其方程右侧的一个实例,从秩至多为 $m$ 的有限多个事实产出;由归纳假设,这些事实都是 $\inherits'$ 之上的事实。五个方程恰在两处读取原始查询,且两处都是正向的:方程~\eqref{eq:overrides} 的守卫 $p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell \in \dom(\inherits)$,以及方程~\eqref{eq:bases} 的绑定子 $(n, w) \in \inherits(p_{\mathrm{override}})$;二者在 $\inherits$ 之上成立时在 $\inherits'$ 之上也成立。因此同一个实例在 $\inherits'$ 之上产出该事实。存在性的断言由 $\supers$ 的包含得到。
\else
We read both least fixed points over the queries of the five functions at all paths; this query set
contains the queries arising from either AST, and by Lemma~\ref{lem:dependency-restriction} the
restriction of a least fixed point to the dependency-closed queries arising from one AST is unchanged
by adding the others. By strong induction on the Kleene rank over $\inherits$
(Lemma~\ref{lem:kleene-iteration}). A fact of rank $m + 1$ is produced by one instance of the right-hand side of its equation from finitely many
facts of rank at most $m$, which are facts over $\inherits'$ by the induction hypothesis. The five
equations read the primitive query in exactly two places, both positive: the guard
$p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell \in \dom(\inherits)$ of equation~\eqref{eq:overrides} and the binder
$(n, w) \in \inherits(p_{\mathrm{override}})$ of equation~\eqref{eq:bases}; both hold over
$\inherits'$ when they hold over $\inherits$. Hence the same instance produces the fact over
$\inherits'$. The existence claim follows from the containment of $\supers$.
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧：组合体按第 7 节定义(P 的文本原样保留、Q 是追加的顶层定义),代理把「承载二者的文档」形式化为根成员表
%   的串接。(i)(ii) 是语法层论证,未机械化(Lean 只有抽象 inherits)。
% 作者侧（与任务书背景的出入,代理 2026-10-17）：任务书说 prop:extensibility-by-construction 的 (iii)「原文档不变」不普遍成立,因为
%   Q 可能在顶层写同名定义。按论文的良构性,同名顶层定义是根处的重复键(情形 2),组合体不良构,被 (U) 的前提排除;
%   对良构的组合体,P 的位置上 inherits 不变(本引理 (ii))。本文只按任务书证明包含,不另证相等。
\begin{lemma}[\bilingual{Composites}{组合体}]\label{lem:composite-extension}
\ifPaperChinese
设 $P$ 是良构程序,$Q$ 是一列标签两两不同的顶层定义,且这些标签都不是 $P$ 的顶层标签;设 $C$ 是它们的组合体(第~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}~节),其根 mixin 依次列出 $P$ 的根的成员与 $Q$ 的成员。则:
(i) $P$ 中书写的每个位置都是 $C$ 的书写位置,其节点不变,且在 $C$ 中该处不出现任何良构性情形;
(ii) $\inherits_C$ 扩展 $\inherits_P$,且对每个 $p \in \dom(\inherits_P)$ 有 $\inherits_C(p) = \inherits_P(p)$;
(iii) 在任意文档中,一个书写位置 $p$ 是否良构只取决于 $p$ 处的节点、$p$ 的各前缀的末尾标签,以及 $p$ 的各外围作用域写下的标签;对 $C$ 中 $Q$ 的位置,根处写下的标签是 $P$ 与 $Q$ 的顶层标签。
若 $Q$ 重用了 $P$ 的一个顶层标签,或把一个标签写了两次,则根带有一个重复键(情形 2),$C$ 不良构。
\else
Let $P$ be a well-formed program and $Q$ a list of top-level definitions with pairwise distinct labels,
none of which is a top-level label of $P$, and let $C$ be their composite (Section~\ref{sec:semantic-variants}), whose
root mixin lists the members of $P$'s root followed by those of $Q$. Then:
(i) every position written in $P$ is a written position of $C$ with the same node, and no case of
well-formedness arises at it in $C$;
(ii) $\inherits_C$ extends $\inherits_P$, with $\inherits_C(p) = \inherits_P(p)$ for every
$p \in \dom(\inherits_P)$;
(iii) in any document, whether a written position $p$ is well formed depends only on the node at $p$,
on the final labels of the prefixes of $p$, and on the labels written at the enclosing scopes of $p$;
for a position of $Q$ in $C$, the labels written at the root are the top-level labels of $P$ and $Q$.
If $Q$ reuses a top-level label of $P$, or writes one label twice, the root carries a duplicate key
(case~2) and $C$ is ill formed.
\fi
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
(iii):四种情形逐一读取。情形 (1)、(4) 只读 $p$ 处的节点。情形 (2) 在 $\at$ 沿 $p$ 的各前缀下降时只读各前缀处成员的键。情形 (3) 的 $\lexical$ 在外围作用域 $q$ 处读 $\has(q, \cdot)$,即 $q$ 处写下的标签,$\qualified$ 读 $\last(q)$;二者都不读任何语义函数。
(i):$C$ 的根列出 $P$ 的全部根成员,且由于 $Q$ 的标签是新鲜的,$\at_C$ 在每个顶层标签处的解是唯一的,并与 $\at_P$ 相同;此后 $\at$ 沿 $P$ 的路径下降时经过的是 $P$ 的节点,故 $P$ 的每个书写位置在 $C$ 中有相同的节点。情形 (1)、(2)、(4) 是节点局部的,在非根节点处不变;在根处,$C$ 的根是一个序列,其成员是 $P$ 的根成员与 $Q$ 的定义,故情形 (4) 成立,且其键两两不同。对情形 (3):$P$ 的一个引用的爬升所经过的外围作用域,除根以外都是 $P$ 的非根节点,它们写下的标签不变;根处写下的标签只是增多了。$P$ 的爬升在 $P$ 中停在某个作用域 $q_0$;在 $C$ 中,它在到达 $q_0$ 之前测试的是同样的非根作用域,因而停在同一个 $q_0$,而 $\qualified$ 只读末尾标签,不受影响。
(ii):由 (i),$\dom(\inherits_P) \subseteq \dom(\inherits_C)$,且对 $p \in \dom(\inherits_P)$,$p$ 处的引用成员相同,每个引用的索引由 (i) 中的爬升论证保持不变,故 $\inherits_C(p) = \inherits_P(p)$。
最后一句:根成员中出现同一个键两次,即情形 (2)。
\else
(iii): we read the four cases. Cases (1) and (4) read only the node at $p$. Case (2) reads, as $\at$
descends along the prefixes of $p$, only the keys of the members at each prefix. In case (3),
$\lexical$ reads $\has(q, \cdot)$ at each enclosing scope $q$, that is, the labels written at $q$, and
$\qualified$ reads $\last(q)$; neither reads any semantic function.
(i): the root of $C$ lists every root member of $P$, and since the labels of $Q$ are fresh, $\at_C$ has
a unique solution at every top-level label, equal to that of $\at_P$; from there $\at$ descends along a
path of $P$ through the nodes of $P$, so every written position of $P$ has the same node in $C$. Cases
(1), (2), and (4) are node-local and unchanged at nonroot nodes; at the root, the root of $C$ is a
sequence whose members are the root members of $P$ and the definitions of $Q$, so case~(4) holds there,
and its keys are pairwise distinct. For
case (3): the enclosing scopes that the climb of a reference of $P$ passes are, apart from the root,
nonroot nodes of $P$, whose written labels are unchanged; at the root the written labels only grow. In
$P$ the climb stops at some scope $q_0$; in $C$ it tests the same nonroot scopes before reaching $q_0$,
so it stops at the same $q_0$, and $\qualified$, which reads final labels only, is unaffected.
(ii): by (i), $\dom(\inherits_P) \subseteq \dom(\inherits_C)$, and for $p \in \dom(\inherits_P)$ the
reference members at $p$ are the same and the index of each is preserved by the climb argument of (i),
so $\inherits_C(p) = \inherits_P(p)$.
The last sentence: the same key occurs twice among the root members, which is case~(2).
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧：「观测」取第 3 节的含义:supers 是 mixin 树的观察式语义,一个读点 q 观测到 supers(q) 的对,存在性
%   是其特例(supers(q) ≠ ∅)。演算没有任何构造读取「某事实不成立」:方程是程序意义的唯一来源,其右侧对子查询
%   全是正向的(lem:monotonicity),对原始量也是正向的(上一条引理)。Lean:fact_upward_closed。
\begin{proposition}[\bilingual{Upward-closed observation}{向上封闭的观测}]\label{prop:upward-closed-observation}
\ifPaperChinese
称一个程序的一条路径 $q$ 为\emph{读点},称 $\supers(q)$ 的各个对为 $q$ 处的\emph{事实};$q$ 存在当且仅当 $q$ 处有一个事实,而观测由这些事实决定。则对每个文档以及其原始查询的每个扩展,每个读点处的每个事实都保持。因此在原始查询的扩展之下,没有事实依赖于另一个事实的缺失:若 $q$ 存在而 $r$ 不存在,则在每个使 $r$ 存在的扩展之上 $q$ 仍然存在。一个读点所表达的每个性质,即使 $q$ 存在、或使某个给定的对属于 $\supers(q)$ 的那类原始查询,在扩展之下都是向上封闭的。一个良构程序与新鲜顶层定义的组合体是其原始查询的扩展(引理~\ref{lem:composite-extension}~(ii));文本的一次编辑是原始查询的扩展,只要它保持每个引用的索引并只增加书写位置与引用,而在一个词法引用所爬经的作用域写下其首标签的定义,会给该引用重新索引。
\else
We call a path $q$ of a program a \emph{read point}, and the pairs of $\supers(q)$ the \emph{facts} at
$q$; $q$ exists exactly when there is a fact at $q$, and the facts determine the observation. Then for
every document and every extension of its primitive query, every fact at every read point persists.
Consequently, under extensions of the primitive query, no fact depends on the absence of another: if
$q$ exists and $r$ does not, then $q$ still exists over every extension in which $r$ exists. Every
property that a read point expresses, the class of primitive queries over which $q$ exists or over
which a given pair belongs to $\supers(q)$, is upward closed under extension. The composite of a
well-formed program with fresh top-level definitions extends its primitive query
(Lemma~\ref{lem:composite-extension}~(ii)); an edit of the text extends the primitive query
when it keeps the index of every reference and only adds written positions and references, while a
definition of the leading label of a lexical reference, written at a scope its climb passes, re-indexes
that reference.
\fi
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
程序的意义恰是 $\lfp(T_P)$(定理~\ref{thm:well-defined}),而读点处的事实是它的事实;由引理~\ref{lem:program-monotonicity},这些事实在扩展之下保持。关于 $q$ 与 $r$ 的那句是其特例:$q$ 的存在性是一个事实,与 $r$ 无关地保持。
\else
The meaning of a program is $\lfp(T_P)$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:well-defined}), and the facts at read points
are its facts; by Lemma~\ref{lem:program-monotonicity} these facts persist under extension. The sentence about
$q$ and $r$ is a special case: the existence of $q$ is a fact, and it persists regardless of $r$.
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧：S 继承 T 时 T 非根,定理需要它(根的 supers 是 {((),())},其书写分量 () 不会出现在别处)。这条用到
%   语法层(限定 this 在 n = 0 时末尾标签有定义),未机械化;Lean 里以 basePath ≠ [] 为假设。
\begin{lemma}[\bilingual{Nonroot targets}{非根目标}]\label{lem:nonroot-targets}
\ifPaperChinese
在一个良构文档中,对每条路径 $q$,$\bases(q)$ 的元素都不是根。
\else
In a well-formed document, no element of $\bases(q)$ is the root, for every path $q$.
\fi
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
先看两个事实。其一,一个顶层路径 $(\ell)$ 的 override 只有它自己:方程~\eqref{eq:overrides} 的分支来自 $\supers(()) = \{((), ())\}$。其二,因此一个 $\supers$ 对 $(p_{\mathrm{site}}, p_{\mathrm{override}})$ 若有 $|p_{\mathrm{override}}| \ge 2$,则 $p_{\mathrm{site}} \neq ()$:位点为根意味着该对来自一个顶层的链节点,其 override 长度为一。
现在一个目标是 $p_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w$,其中 $(n, w) \in \inherits(p_{\mathrm{override}})$ 是写在 $q$ 的某个 override $p_{\mathrm{override}}$ 处的一个引用 $r$ 的索引化,而 $p_{\mathrm{current}} \in \this(\{\init(q)\}, \init(p_{\mathrm{override}}), n)$。词法引用的 $w$ 以其首标签开头,非空。限定 $\this$ 引用 $(\ell, {\sim}, \ell_1, \ldots, \ell_k)$ 在 $k \ge 1$ 时 $w$ 非空;在 $k = 0$ 时,$n = \qualified(\init(p_{\mathrm{override}}), \ell)$ 停在一个末尾标签为 $\ell$ 的作用域,故 $n < m$,其中 $m = |\init(p_{\mathrm{override}})|$。若 $n \ge 1$,最后一步消费的对的 override 分量长度为 $m - n + 1 \ge 2$,由其二,输出的位点非根。若 $n = 0$,目标是 $\init(q)$;若它是根,则 $q$ 是顶层路径,由其一 $p_{\mathrm{override}} = q$,于是 $\init(p_{\mathrm{override}}) = ()$,而 $\qualified((), \ell)$ 无定义,这与良构性矛盾。
\else
Two facts first. First, the overrides of a top-level path $(\ell)$ are that path alone: the branches of
equation~\eqref{eq:overrides} come from $\supers(()) = \{((), ())\}$. Second, therefore a $\supers$
pair $(p_{\mathrm{site}}, p_{\mathrm{override}})$ with $|p_{\mathrm{override}}| \ge 2$ has
$p_{\mathrm{site}} \neq ()$: a root site means that the pair comes from a top-level chain node, whose
override has length one.
Now a target is $p_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w$, where $(n, w) \in \inherits(p_{\mathrm{override}})$ indexes a
reference $r$ written at some override $p_{\mathrm{override}}$ of $q$ and
$p_{\mathrm{current}} \in \this(\{\init(q)\}, \init(p_{\mathrm{override}}), n)$. The $w$ of a lexical
reference begins with its leading label and is nonempty. A qualified-this reference
$(\ell, {\sim}, \ell_1, \ldots, \ell_k)$ has a nonempty $w$ when $k \ge 1$; when $k = 0$,
$n = \qualified(\init(p_{\mathrm{override}}), \ell)$ stops at a scope whose final label is $\ell$, so
$n < m$ where $m = |\init(p_{\mathrm{override}})|$. If $n \ge 1$, the pair consumed by the last step has
an override component of length $m - n + 1 \ge 2$, so by the second fact the output site is nonroot. If
$n = 0$, the target is $\init(q)$; were it the root, $q$ would be top-level, so
$p_{\mathrm{override}} = q$ by the first fact and $\init(p_{\mathrm{override}}) = ()$, where
$\qualified((), \ell)$ is undefined, contradicting well-formedness.
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧（证明结构）：把 prop:extensibility-by-construction 的 (D1)–(D4) 从固定包装 __extend 推广到任意继承位点,并同时推广到两个文档
%   (D 与把若干绝对引用从 T 改指 S 的 D′),于是 (U) 的 (ii) 是 D′ = D 的特例,行为兼容推论是一般情形。
%   (D1)–(D4) 的映射 ψ 在一般情形下不是函数(T 可以是 S 的前缀或反之),这里改用关系「对应」;唯一不能由子推导
%   得到的事实是书写的自反 override pT⌢v ∈ overrides(pS⌢v),由 (G2) 对 v 归纳直接构造。
%   Lean:Substitutability.lean 的 Redirection.simulation(motive 的五个分量即 (E1)–(E4) 加链的情形)。
\begin{lemma}[\bilingual{Redirection simulation}{改指模拟}]\label{lem:redirection-simulation}
\ifPaperChinese
设 $D$ 是一个 $\dom(\inherits)$ 前缀封闭的文档,$p_S$ 是一条路径,$p_T$ 是一条非根路径。称写在路径 $o$ 处的一个引用 $(n, w)$ 为\emph{绝对的},当且仅当 $n = |\init(o)|$,即它爬升到根并从根命名一条路径。设 $D'$ 是一个文档,满足 $\dom(\inherits_D) \subseteq \dom(\inherits_{D'})$ 与 $p_T \in \bases^*_{D'}(p_S)$,并且对每个 $(n, w) \in \inherits_D(o)$,要么 $(n, w) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$,要么该引用是绝对的、$w = p_T \cat u$ 且 $(n, p_S \cat u) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$。当 $a = b$,或对某个后缀 $v$ 有 $a = p_S \cat v$ 且 $b = p_T \cat v$ 时,我们说路径 $a$ \emph{对应}路径 $b$。则对每个 $(s, o) \in \supers_D(q)$ 和每个对应 $q$ 的 $a$,存在 $(s', o') \in \supers_{D'}(a)$,使 $s'$ 对应 $s$、$o'$ 对应 $o$,且当 $o \in \dom(\inherits_D)$ 时 $o' = o$。特别地,对每条路径 $q$ 与每个后缀 $w$,$D$ 在 $q$ 处的书写观测包含于 $D'$ 在 $q$ 处的书写观测,$D$ 在 $p_T \cat w$ 处的书写观测包含于 $D'$ 在 $p_S \cat w$ 处的书写观测,且 $D$ 中 $p_T \cat w$ 的存在蕴涵 $D'$ 中 $p_S \cat w$ 的存在。
\else
Let $D$ be a document whose $\dom(\inherits)$ is prefix-closed, $p_S$ a path, and $p_T$ a nonroot path.
Call a reference $(n, w)$ written at a path $o$ \emph{absolute} when $n = |\init(o)|$, so that it climbs
to the root and names a path from there. Let $D'$ be a document with
$\dom(\inherits_D) \subseteq \dom(\inherits_{D'})$ and $p_T \in \bases^*_{D'}(p_S)$, such that for every
$(n, w) \in \inherits_D(o)$ either $(n, w) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$, or the reference is absolute,
$w = p_T \cat u$, and $(n, p_S \cat u) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$. We say that a path $a$
\emph{corresponds} to a path $b$ when $a = b$, or $a = p_S \cat v$ and $b = p_T \cat v$ for some suffix
$v$. Then for every $(s, o) \in \supers_D(q)$ and every $a$ corresponding to $q$ there is
$(s', o') \in \supers_{D'}(a)$ with $s'$ corresponding to $s$, $o'$ corresponding to $o$, and
$o' = o$ when $o \in \dom(\inherits_D)$. In particular, for every path $q$ and every suffix $w$, the
written observation of $D$ at $q$ is contained in that of $D'$ at $q$, the written observation of $D$ at
$p_T \cat w$ is contained in that of $D'$ at $p_S \cat w$, and the existence of $p_T \cat w$ in $D$ implies that of $p_S \cat w$ in
$D'$.
\fi
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
我们说 $a$ \emph{严格对应} $b$,当且仅当 $a$ 对应 $b$ 且 $(a, b) \neq (p_S, p_T)$,即 $a = b$ 或后缀 $v$ 非空;此时若 $b = b_0 \snoc \ell$,则 $a = a_0 \snoc \ell$ 且 $a_0$ 对应 $b_0$。先看两个基础事实。(G1) $p_S \neq ()$:$\bases(()) = \varnothing$,故从根出发的链停在根,而 $p_T$ 非根。(G2) 对每个使 $p_T \cat v \in \dom(\inherits_D)$ 的 $v$,$\supers_{D'}(p_S \cat v)$ 中有一个形如 $(\cdot,\, p_T \cat v)$ 的对。对 $v$ 归纳:$v = ()$ 时 $p_T$ 是书写的,沿用命题~\ref{prop:extensibility-by-construction} 的存在性归纳,由前缀封闭,每条书写路径都是它自己的 override,故 $(\init(p_T), p_T) \in \supers_{D'}(p_T)$;沿链 $p_S \to^* p_T$ 把这一对带到 $\supers_{D'}(p_S)$($\bases^*$ 是传递的,且由 (G1) $p_S$ 非根)。在 $v \snoc \ell$ 处:$p_T \cat v$ 是书写的(前缀封闭),归纳假设给出分支 $p_T \cat v$ 位于 $\supers_{D'}(p_S \cat v)$ 中,其守卫 $p_T \cat v \snoc \ell \in \dom(\inherits_{D'})$ 成立,故方程~\eqref{eq:overrides} 产出 $p_T \cat v \snoc \ell \in \overrides_{D'}(p_S \cat v \snoc \ell)$,再由自反链得到 $(p_S \cat v,\, p_T \cat v \snoc \ell) \in \supers_{D'}(p_S \cat v \snoc \ell)$。
\else
We say that $a$ \emph{strictly corresponds} to $b$ when $a$ corresponds to $b$ and
$(a, b) \neq (p_S, p_T)$, that is, $a = b$ or the suffix $v$ is nonempty; then $b = b_0 \snoc \ell$
gives $a = a_0 \snoc \ell$ with $a_0$ corresponding to $b_0$. Two ground facts first. (G1)
$p_S \neq ()$: $\bases(()) = \varnothing$, so a chain from the root stays at the root, while $p_T$ is
nonroot. (G2) For every $v$ with $p_T \cat v \in \dom(\inherits_D)$, $\supers_{D'}(p_S \cat v)$ has a
pair of the form $(\cdot,\, p_T \cat v)$. By induction on $v$: at $v = ()$, $p_T$ is written, and as in
the existence induction of Proposition~\ref{prop:extensibility-by-construction}, by prefix closure every written path is its
own override, so $(\init(p_T), p_T) \in \supers_{D'}(p_T)$; the chain $p_S \to^* p_T$ carries this pair
to $\supers_{D'}(p_S)$, since $\bases^*$ is transitive and $p_S$ is nonroot by (G1). At $v \snoc \ell$:
$p_T \cat v$ is written (prefix closure), the induction hypothesis gives the branch $p_T \cat v$ in
$\supers_{D'}(p_S \cat v)$, its guard $p_T \cat v \snoc \ell \in \dom(\inherits_{D'})$ holds, so
equation~\eqref{eq:overrides} produces $p_T \cat v \snoc \ell \in \overrides_{D'}(p_S \cat v \snoc \ell)$,
and the reflexive chain gives
$(p_S \cat v,\, p_T \cat v \snoc \ell) \in \supers_{D'}(p_S \cat v \snoc \ell)$.
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
对给定事实在 $D$ 中的 Kleene 秩作强归纳(每次降秩都来自末步反演,引理~\ref{lem:kleene-iteration}),我们同时证明四个条款。
(E1) $(s, o) \in \supers_D(q)$ 且 $a$ 对应 $q$,蕴涵存在 $(s', o') \in \supers_{D'}(a)$,$s'$ 对应 $s$,$o'$ 对应 $o$,且 $o$ 书写时 $o' = o$。
(E2) $o \in \overrides_D(q)$ 且 $a$ 严格对应 $q$,蕴涵存在 $o' \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$,$o'$ 对应 $o$,且 $o$ 书写时 $o' = o$。
(E3) $t \in \bases_D(q)$ 且 $a$ 严格对应 $q$,蕴涵存在 $t' \in \bases_{D'}(a)$ 对应 $t$;以及从 $q$ 到 $t$ 的一条 $\bases^*_D$ 链且 $a$ 严格对应 $q$,蕴涵存在一条从 $a$ 到某个严格对应 $t$ 的 $t'$ 的 $\bases^*_{D'}$ 链。
(E4) $t \in \this_D(\{s\}, d, n)$,$d$ 书写,且 $s'$ 对应 $s$,蕴涵存在对应 $t$ 的 $t' \in \this_{D'}(\{s'\}, d, n)$。
\else
By strong induction on the Kleene rank in $D$ of the given fact (every rank decrease from last-step
inversion, Lemma~\ref{lem:kleene-iteration}), we prove four clauses simultaneously.
(E1) $(s, o) \in \supers_D(q)$ and $a$ corresponding to $q$ imply some
$(s', o') \in \supers_{D'}(a)$ with $s'$ corresponding to $s$, $o'$ corresponding to $o$, and $o' = o$
when $o$ is written.
(E2) $o \in \overrides_D(q)$ and $a$ strictly corresponding to $q$ imply some
$o' \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$ corresponding to $o$, with $o' = o$ when $o$ is written.
(E3) $t \in \bases_D(q)$ and $a$ strictly corresponding to $q$ imply some $t' \in \bases_{D'}(a)$
corresponding to $t$; and a $\bases^*_D$ chain from $q$ to $t$, with $a$ strictly corresponding to $q$,
implies a $\bases^*_{D'}$ chain from $a$ to some $t'$ strictly corresponding to $t$.
(E4) $t \in \this_D(\{s\}, d, n)$ with $d$ written and $s'$ corresponding to $s$ imply some
$t' \in \this_{D'}(\{s'\}, d, n)$ corresponding to $t$.
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
(E1):$q = ()$ 时 $a = ()$($p_T$ 非根),取根的对。否则反演给出一条链 $q \to^* p_{\mathrm{base}}$ 与 $o \in \overrides_D(p_{\mathrm{base}})$,秩都更小,且 $s = \init(p_{\mathrm{base}})$。若 $a$ 严格对应 $q$,(E3) 给出一条链 $a \to^* p'$,$p'$ 严格对应 $p_{\mathrm{base}}$,(E2) 给出 $o' \in \overrides_{D'}(p')$;于是 $(\init(p'), o') \in \supers_{D'}(a)$,且 $\init(p')$ 对应 $\init(p_{\mathrm{base}})$。若 $(a, q) = (p_S, p_T)$,先对 $(p_T, p_T)$ 照此处理,再在前面接上链 $p_S \to^* p_T$。
\else
(E1): if $q = ()$ then $a = ()$, as $p_T$ is nonroot, and the root pair serves. Otherwise inversion gives
a chain $q \to^* p_{\mathrm{base}}$ and $o \in \overrides_D(p_{\mathrm{base}})$, both of smaller rank,
with $s = \init(p_{\mathrm{base}})$. If $a$ strictly corresponds to $q$, (E3) gives a chain $a \to^* p'$
with $p'$ strictly corresponding to $p_{\mathrm{base}}$, and (E2) gives $o' \in \overrides_{D'}(p')$; so
$(\init(p'), o') \in \supers_{D'}(a)$, and $\init(p')$ corresponds to $\init(p_{\mathrm{base}})$. If
$(a, q) = (p_S, p_T)$, treat the pair $(p_T, p_T)$ in this way and prefix the chain $p_S \to^* p_T$.
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
(E2):$q = ()$ 时 $o = ()$。对 $q = q_0 \snoc \ell$,反演给出一个分支 $(\cdot, p_{\mathrm{branch}}) \in \supers_D(q_0)$,且 $p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell$ 是书写的;由前缀封闭 $p_{\mathrm{branch}}$ 是书写的,于是 (E1) 在 $a_0$ 处给出同一个 $p_{\mathrm{branch}}$ 位于 $\supers_{D'}(a_0)$ 中,而其守卫在 $D'$ 中成立。分支 override $p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell$ 因此在两边都被产出。对自反 override $o = q$:若 $a = q$,它在 $a$ 处被产出;否则 $q = p_T \cat v$,$a = p_S \cat v$,$v$ 非空,此时自反地产出 $o' = a$,它对应 $o$;若 $o$ 是书写的,则由 (G2) 与守卫 $o \in \dom(\inherits_{D'})$,$o$ 本身属于 $\overrides_{D'}(a)$。
\else
(E2): if $q = ()$ then $o = ()$. For $q = q_0 \snoc \ell$, inversion gives a branch
$(\cdot, p_{\mathrm{branch}}) \in \supers_D(q_0)$ with $p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell$ written; by prefix
closure $p_{\mathrm{branch}}$ is written, so (E1) at $a_0$ yields the same $p_{\mathrm{branch}}$ in
$\supers_{D'}(a_0)$, and its guard holds in $D'$. The branch override $p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell$ is
therefore produced on both sides. For the reflexive override $o = q$: if $a = q$ it is produced at $a$;
otherwise $q = p_T \cat v$ and $a = p_S \cat v$ with $v$ nonempty, and the reflexive $o' = a$, which
corresponds to $o$, is produced; if $o$ is written, then by (G2) and the guard
$o \in \dom(\inherits_{D'})$, $o$ itself belongs to $\overrides_{D'}(a)$.
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
(E3):反演给出一个 override $o \in \overrides_D(q)$,它携带 $(n, w) \in \inherits_D(o)$,因而是书写的,以及 $p_{\mathrm{current}} \in \this_D(\{q_0\}, \init(o), n)$,$t = p_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w$。(E2) 给出 $o \in \overrides_{D'}(a)$ 本身;$\init(o)$ 是书写的,(E4) 给出 $p'_{\mathrm{current}} \in \this_{D'}(\{a_0\}, \init(o), n)$,它对应 $p_{\mathrm{current}}$。若 $(n, w) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$,则 $t' = p'_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w$ 对应 $t$,因为对应关系在接上同一个后缀时保持。若该引用被改指,则它是绝对的,$n = |\init(o)|$,$w = p_T \cat u$:从一个非根定义点出发、步数等于其长度的走步停在根(每一步匹配一个短一个标签的 override 分量,而长度为一的 override 分量所在的对以根为位点),而当 $|\init(o)| = 0$ 时 $o$ 是顶层路径,由 $q$ 的 override 是顶层路径知 $q_0$ 是根。故 $p_{\mathrm{current}} = ()$,而对应它的 $p'_{\mathrm{current}}$ 也是 $()$;于是 $t = p_T \cat u$,$t' = p_S \cat u$ 对应 $t$。对链,对其长度归纳:每一跳由前一部分映射;一跳若落在 $t' = p_S$ 而 $t = p_T$,则接上链 $p_S \to^* p_T$,使它落在 $p_T$ 本身,于是终点仍严格对应。
\else
(E3): inversion gives an override $o \in \overrides_D(q)$ carrying $(n, w) \in \inherits_D(o)$, hence
written, and $p_{\mathrm{current}} \in \this_D(\{q_0\}, \init(o), n)$ with
$t = p_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w$. (E2) yields $o$ itself in $\overrides_{D'}(a)$; $\init(o)$ is written,
and (E4) yields $p'_{\mathrm{current}} \in \this_{D'}(\{a_0\}, \init(o), n)$ corresponding to
$p_{\mathrm{current}}$. If $(n, w) \in \inherits_{D'}(o)$, then $t' = p'_{\mathrm{current}} \cat w$
corresponds to $t$, since correspondence is kept when the same suffix is appended. If the reference is
redirected, it is absolute, $n = |\init(o)|$, and $w = p_T \cat u$: a walk from a nonroot definition site
with as many steps as its length ends at the root, since each step matches an override component one
label shorter and a pair whose override component has length one has the root as its site; and when
$|\init(o)| = 0$, $o$ is a top-level path, and since $o$ is an override of $q$, the scope $q_0$ is the
root. So $p_{\mathrm{current}} = ()$, and $p'_{\mathrm{current}}$, which corresponds to it, is $()$ as
well; hence $t = p_T \cat u$, and $t' = p_S \cat u$ corresponds to $t$. For chains, by induction on their
length: each hop is mapped by the first part; a hop landing at $t' = p_S$ with $t = p_T$ is extended by
the chain $p_S \to^* p_T$ so as to land at $p_T$ itself, and the endpoint strictly corresponds.
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
(E4):$n = 0$ 时是平凡的。$n > 0$ 时,反演给出 $(s_1, d) \in \supers_D(s)$ 与 $t \in \this_D(\{s_1\}, \init(d), n - 1)$,秩都更小;$d$ 是书写的,(E1) 在 $s'$ 处给出 $(s'_1, d) \in \supers_{D'}(s')$,$s'_1$ 对应 $s_1$;$\init(d)$ 是书写的,归纳假设完成余下的 $n - 1$ 步。
结论:在 $a = q$ 处与在 $a = p_S \cat w$、$q = p_T \cat w$ 处应用 (E1);存在性由一个对的存在得到。
\else
(E4): the case $n = 0$ is immediate. For $n > 0$, inversion gives $(s_1, d) \in \supers_D(s)$ and
$t \in \this_D(\{s_1\}, \init(d), n - 1)$, both of smaller rank; $d$ is written, so (E1) at $s'$ gives
$(s'_1, d) \in \supers_{D'}(s')$ with $s'_1$ corresponding to $s_1$; $\init(d)$ is written, and the
induction hypothesis covers the remaining $n - 1$ steps.
The conclusions apply (E1) at $a = q$, and at $a = p_S \cat w$ with $q = p_T \cat w$; existence
follows from the existence of a pair.
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧（(U) 的状态）：(a) 与 (b1) 是语法层论证(引理 composite-extension,纸面);(b2) 的语义部分是
%   lem:redirection-simulation 在 D′ = D 时的特例,已机械化(inheritance_substitutable),外加 (b1) 把 P 中的
%   事实搬进 C。不把良构性挪进前提:P 的位置在组合体中保持良构是证出来的(引理 composite-extension (i))。
% 作者侧（第十二轮）:书写观测决定观测这一事实由第 5 节移来,它支撑证明,不是读者理解陈述所需。
\ifPaperChinese
在一个 $\dom(\inherits)$ 前缀封闭的文档里,对一切 $w$ 的 $p \cat w$ 处的书写观测决定 $p$ 处的观测:由引理~\ref{lem:path-existence},$p \cat w \snoc \ell$ 存在当且仅当 $\supers(p \cat w)$ 的某个分支 $p_{\mathrm{branch}}$ 满足 $p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell \in \dom(\inherits)$,而由前缀封闭这样的分支是书写的;且一条路径存在当且仅当它的书写观测非空。
\else
In
a document whose $\dom(\inherits)$ is prefix-closed, the written observations at $p \cat w$ for all $w$
determine the observation at $p$: by Lemma~\ref{lem:path-existence}, $p \cat w \snoc \ell$ exists exactly
when some branch $p_{\mathrm{branch}}$ of $\supers(p \cat w)$ satisfies
$p_{\mathrm{branch}} \snoc \ell \in \dom(\inherits)$, and such a branch is written by prefix closure; and
a path exists exactly when its written observation is nonempty.
\fi

% 作者侧（第十二轮）:thm:inheritance-substitutability 的陈述在第 5 节(sec:modularity),此处只留证明。


\begin{proof}[\bilingual{Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:inheritance-substitutability}}{定理~\ref{thm:inheritance-substitutability} 的证明}]
\ifPaperChinese
(a) 是引理~\ref{lem:composite-extension} 的 (i) 与 (iii);一个 $Q$ 的位置所读取的 $P$ 的内容只是 $P$ 的顶层标签,不含 $P$ 的任何体。(b1) 由引理~\ref{lem:composite-extension}~(ii) 与引理~\ref{lem:program-monotonicity} 得到。对 (b2),若 $p_S = p_T$,结论是平凡的;否则 $p_T$ 是一跳 $\bases$ 的目标,由引理~\ref{lem:nonroot-targets} 非根。语法层产生的文档的 $\dom(\inherits)$ 是前缀封闭的($\at$ 沿前缀递归)。取 $D' = D = C$、不改指任何引用,应用引理~\ref{lem:redirection-simulation}:$C$ 中 $p_T \cat w$ 处的书写观测包含于 $C$ 中 $p_S \cat w$ 处的书写观测;由 (b1),$P$ 中 $p_T \cat w$ 处的书写观测包含于前者,而 $P$ 的书写路径在 $C$ 中仍是书写的。观测的包含由存在性的转移得到。一般情形是同一论证,不经过 (b1)。
\else
(a) is Lemma~\ref{lem:composite-extension}~(i) and (iii); what a position of $Q$ reads of $P$ is the
top-level labels of $P$ only, never a body of $P$. (b1) follows from
Lemma~\ref{lem:composite-extension}~(ii) and Lemma~\ref{lem:program-monotonicity}. For (b2), the claim
is immediate when $p_S = p_T$; otherwise $p_T$ is the target of a $\bases$ hop and is nonroot by
Lemma~\ref{lem:nonroot-targets}. A document the syntax layer produces has a prefix-closed
$\dom(\inherits)$, since $\at$ recurses through prefixes. Lemma~\ref{lem:redirection-simulation} with
$D' = D = C$ and no reference redirected gives that the written observation of $C$ at $p_T \cat w$ is
contained in that of $C$ at $p_S \cat w$; by (b1), the written observation of $P$ at $p_T \cat w$ is
contained in the former, as the written paths of $P$ remain written in $C$. The containment of
observations follows from the transfer of existence. The general case is the same argument without
(b1).
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧（命题收窄,用户 2026-10-19 同意）:可扩展性命题原有的保存条款移到这里,作为定理 (b2) 的实例。
\ifPaperChinese
取 $p_S = (\text{``\_\_extend''})$、$p_T = (\ell_P)$,(b2) 表明命题~\ref{prop:extensibility-by-construction} 的扩展在每条对应路径处都保留 $P$ 的观测:可扩展性给出新的成员,可替换性保证旧的成员都还在。
\else
Taking $p_S = (\text{``\_\_extend''})$ and $p_T = (\ell_P)$, (b2) shows that the extension of
Proposition~\ref{prop:extensibility-by-construction} keeps the observation of $P$ at every
corresponding path: extensibility provides the new member, and substitutability keeps every old one.
\fi

% 作者侧（义务 3 的结果,代理 2026-10-17）：「对一切上下文 C,C[S] 的观测包含 C[T] 的观测」在一般情形下为假;
%   本推论只对【绝对】使用成立。最小见证(已用 Lean 的认证解释器核对,两侧均稳定,证书被接受):
%     m: [t: [x: []], s: [[m, t]], u: [[t]]]      k: [[m], t: [z: []]]
%   C[T] 中 u 经词法引用 [t](索引 0,相对于位点)使用 t;在 k 下,(k, u) 继承的是被 k 覆盖过的 (k, t),
%   故 (k, u, z) 存在。C[S] 把 u 的引用改为 [s]:(k, s) 继承的是 s 以绝对路径 [m, t] 命名的 (m, t),不是 (k, t),
%   故 (k, u, z) 不再存在。S = s 对 T = t 是可替换的((m, s) 拥有 (m, t) 的全部观测),但 (k, s) 并不继承 (k, t):
%   上下文的晚绑定使用看到的是 T 在每个继承点处的像,而 S 只继承它命名的那一个。正文只陈述成立的部分。
%   任务书要建立的前提「上下文只能通过继承 T 或沿路径读取 T 来使用它」按字面不成立:上下文还能继承 T 的外围
%   mixin,把 T 作为成员得到(审查子代理的见证:A: [T: [f: []]],X: [[A]],(X, T, f) 存在而 X 的引用指向 (A))。正文只陈述
%   本推论覆盖的使用(绝对引用与读点)。另:改指在表层要可实现,新首标签不能被爬升经过的非根作用域写下(否则捕获)。
\begin{corollary}[\bilingual{Behavior compatibility}{行为兼容}]\label{cor:behavior-compatibility}
\ifPaperChinese
设 $D$ 是一个良构文档,带有位于 $p_S$ 的 mixin $S$ 与位于非根路径 $p_T$ 的 mixin $T$。本推论关乎一个上下文中命名 $T$ 或其子树中一条路径的绝对引用,以及该子树中的读点;一个上下文还通过指向 $T$ 的外围 mixin 的引用触及 $T$,这类引用把 $T$ 作为成员继承,此处不改指。设 $D'$ 是一个良构文档,其原始查询与 $D$ 的相同,只是一组绝对引用 $(n, p_T \cat u)$ 被改指为 $(n, p_S \cat u)$,并且在 $D'$ 中 $p_T \in \bases^*(p_S)$,例如当 $S$ 以一个不在改指之列的引用继承 $T$、并写下另外的成员时。在表层,一个绝对引用是一个词法引用,其首标签写在根处,且不写在其爬升经过的其他任何作用域处(限定 $\this$ 引用停在一个非根作用域,从不是绝对的);把其投影列表开头的 $p_T$ 的标签改写为 $p_S$ 的标签,当爬升经过的作用域中除根以外没有一个写下新的首标签时,就得到这样的 $D'$。则对每条路径 $q$,$D$ 在 $q$ 处的书写观测包含于 $D'$ 在 $q$ 处的书写观测,且对每个后缀 $w$,$D$ 在 $p_T \cat w$ 处的书写观测包含于 $D'$ 在 $p_S \cat w$ 处的书写观测。因此在一个上下文的绝对使用处以 $S$ 替换 $T$,并在上下文读取 $T$ 之处读取 $S$,不丢失任何观测。
\else
Let $D$ be a well-formed document with a mixin $S$ at $p_S$ and a mixin $T$ at a nonroot path $p_T$. The
corollary concerns the absolute references of a context that name $T$ or a path in its subtree, and
the read points of the context in that subtree; a context also reaches $T$ through references to
enclosing mixins of $T$, which inherit $T$ as a member and are not redirected here. Let $D'$ be a
well-formed document whose primitive query is that of $D$ except that a set of absolute references
$(n, p_T \cat u)$ is redirected to $(n, p_S \cat u)$, such that $p_T \in \bases^*(p_S)$ in $D'$, for
instance when $S$ inherits $T$ through a reference not among those redirected and writes further
members. At the surface, an absolute reference is a lexical reference whose leading label is written at
the root and at no other scope its climb passes (a qualified-this reference stops at a nonroot scope and
is never absolute); rewriting the labels of $p_T$ at the head of its projection list to those of $p_S$
yields such a $D'$ when no scope the climb passes, other than the root, writes the new leading label.
Then for every path $q$ the written observation of $D$ at $q$ is contained in that of $D'$ at $q$, and
for every suffix $w$ the written observation of $D$ at $p_T \cat w$ is contained in that of $D'$ at
$p_S \cat w$. Hence replacing $T$ by $S$ at the absolute uses of a context, and reading $S$ where the
context read $T$, loses no observation.
\fi
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
引理~\ref{lem:redirection-simulation};$D$ 的书写位置在 $D'$ 中仍是书写的,因为改指只改引用。
\else
Lemma~\ref{lem:redirection-simulation}; the written positions of $D$ remain written in $D'$, since
redirection changes references only.
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧（义务 4,代理 2026-10-17）：Mikhajlov–Sekerinski 的 flexibility 性质这里只讲加法修订 C′ = C ⊕ Δ。
%   方向:他们的「C 被 C′ 精化」读作「C′ 在对应路径处拥有 C 的全部观测」(obs(C) ⊆ obs(C′)),即 C′ 成立 C 所成立的
%   每个可观测性质;观测都是向上封闭的正事实(prop:upward-closed-observation),故这与包含同向。
%   结论不需要交换律与结合律:bases* 是传递的,M mod C′ 经 C′ 传递地继承 C,定理 (b2) 直接给出;任务书提示的路线
%   (用交换律、结合律把 M mod C′ 改写为 (M mod C) ⊕ Δ)可行但多绕一步,且需要跨两个结合律实例比较。
%   更强的 M mod C ⊑ M mod C′ 来自行为兼容推论,要求 M 以绝对引用继承 C。
% 作者侧（claim-audit 第 1 轮后,代理 2026-10-17）：在包含读法下 flexibility 的结论不用其前提,对每个继承 C 的修订都成立;
%   而 M 与 Δ 都写下的标签会被深度合并(审查的例子:M 写 flag: [True: []],Δ 写 flag: [False: []],则 M mod C′ 的 flag
%   同时有 True 与 False),这在包含读法下算精化。正文据实写明这两点,不声称解决了 Mikhajlov–Sekerinski 的行为精化问题。
\begin{corollary}[\bilingual{Fragile base class}{脆弱基类}]\label{cor:fragile-base-class}
\ifPaperChinese
Mikhajlov 与 Sekerinski~\cite{mikhajlov1998-fragile-base-class} 的 flexibility 性质要求:若一个基类 $C$ 被其修订 $C'$ 精化,且被扩展 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ 精化,则 $C$ 被 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 精化。我们把``$X$ 被 $Y$ 精化''读作在对应路径处 $X$ 的观测包含于 $Y$ 的观测。取一个良构文档,其中位于 $p_C$ 的 $C$ 与位于 $p_\Delta$ 的一个 mixin 在一个共同的外围作用域中命名;加法修订 $C'$ 是深度合并 \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[[|$p_C$|], [|$p_\Delta$|]]}(定义~\ref{def:merge});$M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ 是一个 mixin,其引用中有一个解析到 $p_C$ 的绝对引用,并写下 $M$ 的成员;并设 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 是同一个 mixin,其中该引用如推论~\ref{cor:behavior-compatibility} 那样改指到 $C'$,且该改指在表层可实现。则 $C$ 被 $C'$ 与 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ 精化,因为二者都继承 $C$;$C$ 被 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 精化,因为 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 经 $C'$ 传递地继承 $C$;并且 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ 被 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 精化。在这一读法下,flexibility 性质的结论不使用其前提:它对每个继承 $C$ 的修订 $C'$ 都成立。包含允许在 $M$ 与 $\Delta$ 都写下的标签处发生深度合并:在 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 中,这样的标签同时带有两处定义。
\else
The flexibility property of Mikhajlov and Sekerinski~\cite{mikhajlov1998-fragile-base-class} requires
that whenever a base class $C$ is refined by its revision $C'$ and by an extension $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$, the base
class $C$ is refined by $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$. We read ``$X$ is refined by $Y$'' as containment of the
observation of $X$ in that of $Y$ at corresponding paths. Take a well-formed document in which $C$ at
$p_C$ and a mixin at $p_\Delta$ are named in a common enclosing scope; the additive revision $C'$ is the
deep merge \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[[|$p_C$|], [|$p_\Delta$|]]} (Definition~\ref{def:merge});
$M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ is a mixin whose references include an absolute one that resolves to $p_C$, and
which writes the members of $M$; and let $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ be the same mixin with that reference
redirected to $C'$ as in Corollary~\ref{cor:behavior-compatibility}, the redirection being realizable at
the surface. Then $C$ is refined by $C'$ and by $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$, since both inherit $C$; $C$ is
refined by $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$, since $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ inherits $C$ transitively through $C'$; and
$M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ is refined by $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$. In this reading the conclusion of the flexibility
property does not use its premises: it holds for every revision $C'$ that inherits $C$. Containment
admits deep merges at labels that $M$ and $\Delta$ both write: in $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ such a label carries
both definitions.
\fi
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
前三条精化是定理~\ref{thm:inheritance-substitutability} 的一般情形,因为 $\bases^*$ 是传递的。最后一条是推论~\ref{cor:behavior-compatibility},把 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ 指向 $C$ 的绝对引用改指为指向 $C'$,而 $C'$ 指向 $C$ 的引用不在改指之列。关于前提的那句:上述链只用到 $C'$ 继承 $C$。关于深度合并的那句:若 $M$ 与 $\Delta$ 都写下标签 $\ell$,则二者的定义都是 $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ 处 $\ell$ 的分支 override(方程~\eqref{eq:overrides})。
\else
The first three refinements are the general case of Theorem~\ref{thm:inheritance-substitutability},
since $\bases^*$ is transitive. The last is Corollary~\ref{cor:behavior-compatibility}, redirecting the
absolute reference of $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C$ from $C$ to $C'$, while the reference of $C'$ to $C$ is not
redirected. The sentence on the premises: the chains above use only that $C'$ inherits $C$. The
sentence on deep merges: if $M$ and $\Delta$ both write a label $\ell$, both definitions are branch
overrides of $\ell$ at $M \mathbin{\mathrm{mod}} C'$ (equation~\eqref{eq:overrides}).
\fi
\end{proof}

\section{\bilingual{Adequacy for the Lazy \texorpdfstring{$\lambda$}{λ}-Calculus: Proofs}{惰性 $\lambda$-演算的充分性：证明}}
\label{app:levy-longo-tree-proofs}

\ifPaperChinese
本附录证明与 $\lambda$-演算对应的继承演算子语言关于惰性
$\lambda$-演算是充分的（adequate，第~\ref{sec:levy-longo-tree}~节）：
一个闭合 $\lambda$-项有弱头范式当且仅当其翻译收敛（定理~\ref{thm:adequacy}）。
\else
This appendix proves that the sublanguage of
inheritance-calculus corresponding to the $\lambda$-calculus
is adequate for the lazy $\lambda$-calculus
(Section~\ref{sec:levy-longo-tree}):
a closed $\lambda$-term has a weak head normal form if and only
if its translation converges (Theorem~\ref{thm:adequacy}).
\fi

\subsection{\bilingual{Weak Head Normal Form}{弱头范式}}

\ifPaperChinese
我们简要回顾弱头归约与 Lévy--Longo 树~\cite{levy1978-reductions-lambda-calcul, longo1983-set-theoretical-models}。
\emph{弱头归约} $M \to_h M'$ 沿函数脊收缩最左的 $\beta$-redex，但不进入抽象体：
若 $M$ 的形式为 $(\lambda x.\, e)\; v\; M_1 \cdots M_k$，则
$M \to_h e[v/x]\; M_1 \cdots M_k$。
若项 $M$ 没有弱头 redex，则称其处于\emph{弱头范式}（WHNF），
即一个抽象 $\lambda x.\, M'$，或一个以变量为首的应用 $y\; M_1 \cdots M_k$。
\else
We briefly recall weak-head reduction and the
L\'evy--Longo tree~\cite{levy1978-reductions-lambda-calcul, longo1983-set-theoretical-models}.
A \emph{weak-head reduction} $M \to_h M'$ contracts the
leftmost $\beta$-redex along the function spine, without
reducing under an abstraction: if $M$ has the form
$(\lambda x.\, e)\; v\; M_1 \cdots M_k$, then
$M \to_h e[v/x]\; M_1 \cdots M_k$.
A term $M$ is in \emph{weak head normal form} (WHNF) if it has
no weak-head redex, that is, $M$ is an abstraction
$\lambda x.\, M'$ or an application $y\; M_1 \cdots M_k$ headed
by a variable.
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
$\lambda$-项 $M$ 的 \emph{Lévy--Longo 树} $\mathrm{LLT}(M)$ 是其遗传式弱头范式：暴露弱头范式的顶层构造子，递归进入直接子项，并在到不了弱头范式之处放上 $\bot$。其根为 $\bot$ 当且仅当 $M$ 没有弱头范式；Lévy--Longo 树是惰性 $\lambda$-演算的标准树语义~\cite{abramsky1993-full-abstraction-lazy-lambda}。
\else
The \emph{L\'evy--Longo tree} $\mathrm{LLT}(M)$ of a
$\lambda$-term $M$ is its hereditary weak head normal form:
expose the top constructor of the WHNF, recurse into the
immediate sub-terms, and place $\bot$ where no WHNF is reached.
Its root is $\bot$ exactly when $M$ has no WHNF; the
L\'evy--Longo tree is the standard tree semantics of the lazy
$\lambda$-calculus~\cite{abramsky1993-full-abstraction-lazy-lambda}.
\fi

% 作者侧注释：路径编码重写以适应纯λ-演算的三条规则。
% 变量现在按名字引用，不使用de Bruijn索引。应用规则推广到任意M1 M2。
% 结果追踪步骤现在只对应两个规则：应用（call）和可选的嵌套应用链。
\subsection{\bilingual{Path Encoding}{路径编码}}

\ifPaperChinese
我们定义 Lévy--Longo 树中的位置与 mixin 树中的路径之间的对应关系。
当翻译 $\mathcal{T}$ 作用于一个纯 $\lambda$-项时，所得 mixin 树具有特定形状：
\else
We define a correspondence between positions in the L\'evy--Longo tree
and paths in the mixin tree.
When the translation $\mathcal{T}$ is applied to a pure
$\lambda$-term, the resulting mixin tree has a specific shape:
\fi
\begin{itemize}
  \item \ifPaperChinese
    抽象 $\lambda x.\, M$ 翻译为一条仅有单一自有定义 $\olbl{__whnf}$ 的 mixin；$\olbl{__whnf}$ 之下的抽象形状具有 label $\{x, \text{``\_\_parameter''}, \text{``\_\_result''}\}$，其中 $x$ 是别名，$\olbl{__parameter}$ 扮演形参声明，$\olbl{__result}$ 持有体。其下 $\olbl{__parameter}$ 有定义的节点称之为\emph{抽象形状}。
    \else
    An abstraction $\lambda x.\, M$ translates to a mixin with a
    single own definition $\olbl{__whnf}$, under which the abstraction
    shape has labels $\{x, \text{``\_\_parameter''}, \text{``\_\_result''}\}$,
    where $x$ is an alias, $\olbl{__parameter}$ plays the role of a
    parameter declaration, and $\olbl{__result}$ holds the body.
    A node under which $\olbl{__parameter}$ is defined
    is referred to as the \emph{abstraction shape}.
    \fi
  \item \ifPaperChinese
    应用 $M_1\; M_2$ 翻译为一条具有自有定义
    $\{\text{``\_\_callee''}, \text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}\}$ 的 mixin，其中
    $\olbl{__callee}$ 命名被调用者 $\mathcal{T}(M_1)$，$\olbl{__call}$
    继承 \mintinline{yaml}{[__callee, __whnf]} 并以 $\mathcal{T}(M_2)$ 覆写 $\olbl{__parameter}$，
    $\olbl{__whnf}$ 投影 \mintinline{yaml}{[__call, __result, __whnf]} 以沿规约链行走。
    \else
    \begin{sloppypar}
    An application $M_1\; M_2$ translates to a mixin with own
    definitions $\{\text{``\_\_callee''},\allowbreak \text{``\_\_call''},\allowbreak \text{``\_\_whnf''}\}$, where
    $\olbl{__callee}$ names the operator $\mathcal{T}(M_1)$,
    $\olbl{__call}$ inherits \mintinline{yaml}{[__callee, __whnf]} and
    overrides $\olbl{__parameter}$ with $\mathcal{T}(M_2)$,
    and $\olbl{__whnf}$ projects
    \mintinline{yaml}{[__call, __result, __whnf]} to walk the reduction chain.
    \end{sloppypar}
    \fi
  \item \ifPaperChinese
    变量 $x$ 翻译为单成员 mixin \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[[|$x$|]]}，其成员是词法引用 \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[|$x$|]}，在翻译后的树中解析为绑定 $x$ 的最近抽象的别名。
    \else
    A variable $x$ translates to the one-member mixin \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[[|$x$|]]},
    whose member is the lexical reference \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[|$x$|]} that resolves in
    the translated tree to the alias of the nearest
    enclosing abstraction that binds $x$.
    \fi
\end{itemize}

\noindent
\ifPaperChinese
Lévy--Longo 树中的每个位置都是从根出发的一系列导航步骤。
在节点 $\lambda x.\, M'$（一个抽象）或 $y\; M_1 \cdots M_k$（以变量为首）处，
可能的步骤为：进入抽象的函数体，以及进入首变量的第 $i$ 个参数 $M_i$。
在 mixin 树中，这些步骤分别对应于跟随 $\olbl{__result}$ 标签（进入函数体）
和在别名解析后跟随 $\olbl{__parameter}$ 标签（进入参数位置）。
\else
Each L\'evy--Longo tree position is a sequence of navigation
steps from the root.
At a node $\lambda x.\, M'$ (an abstraction) or
$y\; M_1 \cdots M_k$ (headed by a variable), the possible steps
are: entering the body of the abstraction, and
entering the $i$-th argument $M_i$ of the head variable.
In the mixin tree, these correspond to following the
$\olbl{__result}$ label (for entering the body) and the
$\olbl{__parameter}$ label after alias resolution (for
entering an argument position).
\fi

\ifPaperChinese
与其在不同 mixin 树之间比较绝对路径（这对内部结构敏感），
我们只观测通过 $\olbl{__whnf}$ 投影可访问的\emph{收敛}行为
（定义~\ref{def:convergence}）。
$\olbl{__whnf}$ 投影继承规约链：沿 $\bases^*$ 的每一跳即一次弱头归约步骤，
因为应用的 $\olbl{__whnf}$ 投影 $(\text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_result''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''})$，
透过 $\olbl{__call}$ 节点的继承收缩 head redex。
链末尾的抽象形状表明已到达弱头范式。
\else
Rather than comparing absolute paths across different mixin
trees, which would be sensitive to internal structure, we
observe only the \emph{convergence} behavior accessible via
the $\olbl{__whnf}$ projection
(Definition~\ref{def:convergence}).
The $\olbl{__whnf}$ projection inherits the reduction chain:
each hop along $\bases^*$ is one weak-head reduction step,
since an application's $\olbl{__whnf}$ projects
$(\text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_result''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''})$,
contracting the head redex through the inheritance at the
$\olbl{__call}$ node.
The abstraction shape at the end of the chain signals that a
weak head normal form has been reached.
\fi

% 作者侧（节点分类引理，2026-07 修复轮新增）：把上面三条形状描述收紧为逐节点的
%   完备分类，供全附录逐情形计算使用；同时给出"书写引用"的 de Bruijn 数据。
%   旧 lem:single-path 的"求值期间的调用"未定义问题由此处的引用起点概念消解：
%   后续引理直接对书写引用及其解析步量化，不再诉诸求值轨迹。
\begin{lemma}[\bilingual{Node classification}{节点分类}]\label{lem:node-classification}
  \ifPaperChinese
  设 $M_0$ 为闭合 $\lambda$-项,$P = \mathcal{T}(M_0)$。
  则 $P$ 的每个书写节点(即 $\dom(\inherits)$ 中的位置及其成员)恰属于下列八类之一,
  且每个书写节点至多携带一条引用:
  \begin{enumerate}
    \item \emph{应用节点}:自有 label $\{\text{``\_\_callee''}, \text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}\}$,无引用;
    \item \emph{redex 节点}(应用之下的 $\olbl{__call}$):一条引用 \mintinline{yaml}{[__callee, __whnf]},
      $\lexical$ 为其构建的 de~Bruijn 索引引用为 $(0,\; (\text{``\_\_callee''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}))$,另有自有 label $\olbl{__parameter}$;
    \item \emph{链节点}(应用之下的 $\olbl{__whnf}$):一条引用 \mintinline{yaml}{[__call, __result, __whnf]},
      $\lexical$ 为其构建的 de~Bruijn 索引引用为 $(0,\; (\text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_result''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}))$,无自有 label;
    \item \emph{抽象节点}:自有 label $\{\text{``\_\_whnf''}\}$,无引用;
    \item \emph{抽象形状}(抽象之下的 $\olbl{__whnf}$):自有 label $\{x, \text{``\_\_parameter''}, \text{``\_\_result''}\}$,无引用;
    \item \emph{别名节点}(形状之下的 $x$):一条引用 \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[parameter]},
      $\lexical$ 为其构建的 de~Bruijn 索引引用为 $(0,\; (\text{``\_\_parameter''}))$,因为形状自身即定义 $\olbl{__parameter}$ 的最近外围作用域;
    \item \emph{形参声明}(形状之下的 $\olbl{__parameter}$):无成员;
    \item \emph{变量节点}($\mathcal{T}(x)$ 的像):一条引用 \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[|$x$|]},
      $\lexical$ 为其构建的 de~Bruijn 索引引用为 $(n,\; (x))$,其中 $n$ 为从该节点的外围作用域 $\init$-上行、
      直到绑定 $x$ 的抽象形状所经过的作用域层数。
  \end{enumerate}
  子项的像(体、被调用者、实参)各居于 $\olbl{__result}$、$\olbl{__callee}$、
  redex 节点的 $\olbl{__parameter}$ 覆写之下,递归地属于第 1、4、8 类。
  特别地,书写引用的\emph{起点}(origin)只会是 redex 节点、链节点、别名节点或变量节点,
  而变量引用的 $\init$-上行只经过第 1、2、4、5 类节点与各子项槽位,
  绝不以形参声明或别名节点为其定义点作用域。
  \else
  Let $M_0$ be a closed $\lambda$-term and $P = \mathcal{T}(M_0)$.
  Every written node of $P$ (the positions in $\dom(\inherits)$ and
  their members) belongs to exactly one of the following eight
  classes, and every written node carries at most one reference:
  \begin{enumerate}
    \item an \emph{application node}: own labels
      $\{\text{``\_\_callee''}, \text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}\}$, no reference;
    \item a \emph{redex node} (the $\olbl{__call}$ of an application):
      one reference \mintinline{yaml}{[__callee, __whnf]}, for which $\lexical$ builds the
      de-Bruijn-indexed reference $(0,\; (\text{``\_\_callee''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}))$, plus the own label
      $\olbl{__parameter}$;
    \item a \emph{chain node} (the $\olbl{__whnf}$ of an application):
      one reference \mintinline{yaml}{[__call, __result, __whnf]}, for which $\lexical$
      builds the de-Bruijn-indexed reference $(0,\; (\text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_result''}, \text{``\_\_whnf''}))$, no own labels;
    \item an \emph{abstraction node}: the single own label
      $\{\text{``\_\_whnf''}\}$, no reference;
    \item \begin{sloppypar}an \emph{abstraction shape} (the $\olbl{__whnf}$ of an
      abstraction): own labels $\{x, \text{``\_\_parameter''}, \text{``\_\_result''}\}$,
      no reference;\end{sloppypar}
    \item an \emph{alias node} (the $x$ of a shape): one reference
      \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[parameter]}, for which $\lexical$ builds the
      de-Bruijn-indexed reference $(0,\; (\text{``\_\_parameter''}))$, because the shape itself is the
      nearest enclosing scope defining $\olbl{__parameter}$;
    \item a \emph{parameter declaration} (the $\olbl{__parameter}$ of a
      shape): no members;
    \item a \emph{variable node} (the image of $\mathcal{T}(x)$): one
      reference \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[|$x$|]}, for which $\lexical$ builds the
      de-Bruijn-indexed reference $(n,\; (x))$, where $n$ is the number of scope levels climbed by
      $\init$ from the node's enclosing scope up to the abstraction
      shape binding $x$.
  \end{enumerate}
  The images of sub-terms (body, operator, argument) live under
  $\olbl{__result}$, under $\olbl{__callee}$, and under the redex node's
  $\olbl{__parameter}$ override, and belong recursively to
  classes~1, 4, and~8.
  In particular, the \emph{origin} of a written reference is always a
  redex node, a chain node, an alias node, or a variable node, and the
  $\init$-climb of a variable reference passes only through nodes of
  classes~1, 2, 4, 5 and the sub-term slots; it never has a parameter
  declaration or an alias node as its definition-site scope.
  \fi
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
  对 $\mathcal{T}$ 的三条规则作结构归纳。三条规则各自书写的节点恰为上列类别
  (应用规则产出第 1、2、3 类,抽象规则产出第 4、5、6、7 类,变量规则产出第 8 类),
  且每条规则在每个节点处至多写下一条引用。
  别名引用的 de~Bruijn 索引:词法引用 \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[parameter]} 自
  $\init(\text{别名节点}) = \text{形状}$ 起测试 $\has(\cdot, \text{``\_\_parameter''})$,
  形状自有 $\olbl{__parameter}$,故 $n = 0$。
  变量引用的 de~Bruijn 索引同理:自 $\init(\text{变量节点})$ 起逐层上行,首个定义 label~$x$
  的作用域是绑定 $x$ 的抽象形状(合成 label 与用户变量名不相交),
  故 $n$ 即所经作用域层数。
  最后一句:引用只出现在第 2、3、6、8 类节点(逐规则检查);
  变量节点的 $\init$-祖先是包含它的槽位与第 1、2、4、5 类节点,
  而形参声明(第 7 类,无成员)与别名节点(第 6 类,其成员只是引用)之下不含任何变量节点,
  故它们不会作为任何变量引用的定义点作用域出现。
\else
  By structural induction on the three rules of $\mathcal{T}$. The
  nodes written by each rule are exactly the classes listed (the
  application rule produces classes~1, 2, 3, the abstraction rule
  classes~4, 5, 6, 7, and the variable rule class~8), and each rule
  writes at most one reference per node.
  For the alias reference, $\lexical$ builds the index as follows: the lexical reference
  \mintinline[escapeinside=||]{yaml}{[parameter]} tests
  $\has(\cdot, \text{``\_\_parameter''})$ outward from
  $\init(\text{alias}) = \text{the shape}$, and the shape owns
  $\olbl{__parameter}$, so $n = 0$.
  For the variable reference likewise: the climb from
  $\init(\text{variable node})$ stops at the first scope defining
  label~$x$, which is the abstraction shape binding $x$ (synthetic
  labels are disjoint from user variable names), so $n$ is the number of scope levels
  passed.
  For the final sentence: references occur only at nodes of
  classes~2, 3, 6, 8 (check per rule); the $\init$-ancestors of a
  variable node are the slots containing it and nodes of
  classes~1, 2, 4, 5, while a parameter declaration (class~7, no
  members) and an alias node (class~6, whose only member is a
  reference) contain no variable node beneath them, so neither ever
  occurs as the definition-site scope of a variable reference.
\fi
\end{proof}

% 作者侧（第十二轮）:def:convergence 移到第 4 节正文;原「Convergence」小节只含此定义,随之删去。


\subsection{\bilingual{The Call-by-Name Machine}{Call-by-Name 机}}

\ifPaperChinese
收敛的充分性的证明把 $P = \mathcal{T}(M_0)$ 的收敛观测与一台标准的
call-by-name 求值机联系起来。这台机器只是叙述词汇:它的每个配置都将被
解码为\emph{同一棵} mixin 树 $P$ 中已经存在的位置,每条迁移都对应对
方程~(\ref{eq:supers})--(\ref{eq:this}) 的一次直接计算;
全部证明自始至终只涉及这一个程序的一个最小不动点。
\else
The proof of adequacy for convergence relates the convergence observation of
$P = \mathcal{T}(M_0)$ to a standard call-by-name evaluation machine.
The machine is presentation vocabulary only: each of its
configurations will be decoded to a position already present in the
\emph{one} mixin tree $P$, and each of its transitions corresponds to
one direct calculation with
equations~(\ref{eq:supers})--(\ref{eq:this}); throughout, the proofs
concern a single least fixed point of this single program.
\fi

\begin{definition}[\bilingual{Krivine machine~\cite{krivine2007-callbyname-machine}}{Krivine 机~\cite{krivine2007-callbyname-machine}}]\label{def:krivine}
  \ifPaperChinese
  固定闭合 $\lambda$-项 $M_0$。\emph{配置}为三元组
  $\langle u,\; \rho,\; S \rangle$,其中 $u$ 是 $M_0$ 的一个子项出现,
  \emph{环境} $\rho$ 把 $u$ 的每个自由变量映射到一个\emph{闭包}
  (一个子项出现与其自身环境的对),\emph{栈} $S$ 是闭包的有限列表。
  迁移规则:
  \begin{align*}
    \langle u_1\, u_2,\; \rho,\; S \rangle
      &\longrightarrow \langle u_1,\; \rho,\; (u_2, \rho) \cdot S \rangle
      && \text{(push)} \\
    \langle \lambda x.\, u',\; \rho,\; c \cdot S \rangle
      &\longrightarrow \langle u',\; \rho[x \mapsto c],\; S \rangle
      && \text{($\beta$)} \\
    \langle x,\; \rho,\; S \rangle
      &\longrightarrow \langle \rho(x).\mathrm{term},\; \rho(x).\mathrm{env},\; S \rangle
      && \text{(lookup)}
  \end{align*}
  初始配置为 $\langle M_0,\; \varnothing,\; \varnothing \rangle$;
  \emph{停机}配置为栈空的抽象 $\langle \lambda x.\, u',\; \rho,\; \varnothing \rangle$。
  因 $M_0$ 闭合,lookup 恒有定义,且停机配置是唯一无后继的配置。
  \else
  Fix a closed $\lambda$-term $M_0$. A \emph{configuration} is a
  triple $\langle u,\; \rho,\; S \rangle$ where $u$ is an occurrence
  of a sub-term of $M_0$, the \emph{environment} $\rho$ maps each free
  variable of $u$ to a \emph{closure} (a pair of a sub-term occurrence
  and its own environment), and the \emph{stack} $S$ is a finite list
  of closures. The transitions are:
  \begin{align*}
    \langle u_1\, u_2,\; \rho,\; S \rangle
      &\longrightarrow \langle u_1,\; \rho,\; (u_2, \rho) \cdot S \rangle
      && \text{(push)} \\
    \langle \lambda x.\, u',\; \rho,\; c \cdot S \rangle
      &\longrightarrow \langle u',\; \rho[x \mapsto c],\; S \rangle
      && \text{($\beta$)} \\
    \langle x,\; \rho,\; S \rangle
      &\longrightarrow \langle \rho(x).\mathrm{term},\; \rho(x).\mathrm{env},\; S \rangle
      && \text{(lookup)}
  \end{align*}
  The initial configuration is
  $\langle M_0,\; \varnothing,\; \varnothing \rangle$; a \emph{halting}
  configuration is an abstraction under the empty stack,
  $\langle \lambda x.\, u',\; \rho,\; \varnothing \rangle$. Since
  $M_0$ is closed, lookup is always defined, and the halting
  configurations are exactly the configurations without a successor.
  \fi
\end{definition}

\begin{proposition}[\bilingual{Machine correctness}{机器正确性}]\label{prop:krivine-whnf}
  \ifPaperChinese
  闭合 $\lambda$-项 $M_0$ 有弱头范式,当且仅当 Krivine 机自
  $\langle M_0, \varnothing, \varnothing \rangle$ 起的运行停机;
  运行中的每次 $\beta$-迁移对应一步弱头归约 $\to_h$,
  而 push 与 lookup 是管理步。
  \else
  A closed $\lambda$-term $M_0$ has a weak head normal form if and
  only if the Krivine machine run from
  $\langle M_0, \varnothing, \varnothing \rangle$ halts; each
  $\beta$-transition of the run corresponds to one weak-head reduction
  step $\to_h$, while push and lookup are administrative.
  \fi
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
\ifPaperChinese
  这是 Krivine 机对 call-by-name 弱头归约的标准正确性
  ~\cite{krivine2007-callbyname-machine}:把配置读回为
  $\lambda$-项(对控制项施行环境替换,再依次应用栈中各闭包的读回),
  则 push 与 lookup 保持读回不变,$\beta$-迁移把读回按 $\to_h$ 收缩一步;
  闭合项的弱头范式必为抽象,恰对应栈空的抽象配置。
\else
  This is the standard correctness of the Krivine machine for
  call-by-name weak-head
  reduction~\cite{krivine2007-callbyname-machine}: reading back a
  configuration as a $\lambda$-term (apply the environment
  substitution to the control, then apply the readbacks of the stacked
  closures in order), push and lookup preserve the readback, and a
  $\beta$-transition contracts it by one $\to_h$ step; the weak head
  normal form of a closed term is an abstraction, which corresponds
  exactly to a halting configuration.
\fi
\end{proof}

\subsection{\bilingual{Machine Simulation}{机器模拟}}

% 作者侧（2026-07 修复轮：本小节取代旧的"替换引理"与"收敛保持"两小节）。旧证明的病根：
%   把 redex 程序与 reduct 程序 T(M[V/x]) 当作"同一程序中同一位置的同一像"跨程序比较
%   （字面为假——reduct 程序根本不出现在含 redex 的闭合程序里），旧 lem:locality 在前提
%   不满足处被引用，结构归纳的 IH 对不上实例。新架构：固定单一程序 P = T(M0)，把机器
%   配置解码为 P 中已存在的位置；替换从不发生（redex = call 节点，reduct = (call,result)
%   子树，收缩一次 head redex = 投影一层）。第二个程序、跨不动点比较、替换引理全部消失。
% 作者侧（度量警告）：旧证明"非自反 bases 跳至少多一层 T_P 迭代"的严格降秩论证不成立——
%   按 def:tp-operator，bases* 闭包在一次 T_P 应用内装配，链长不消耗阶段；链尾对与全链对
%   可以同秩。正确的良基性来自 lem:kleene-iteration 的末步反演（前提严格降秩），
%   归纳陈述必须取分解形式（对链的 bases 事实族归纳），见 lem:sim-backward。
\ifPaperChinese
本小节把 Krivine 机的每个可达配置解码为 $P = \mathcal{T}(M_0)$ 中的路径。
关键观察:翻译从不执行 $\beta$-替换。redex 就是 $\olbl{__call}$ 节点
(它经 \mintinline{yaml}{[__callee, __whnf]} 继承被调用者的抽象形状并覆写
$\olbl{__parameter}$),reduct 就是其下的 $(\text{``\_\_call''}, \text{``\_\_result''})$
